===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\app\api\appointments\track\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { appointmentStore } from '@/services/appointmentService';
import { normalizeAndValidateEgyptianPhone, maskPatientName } from '@/lib/rules';
import { checkIpRateLimit, checkRateLimit, verifyOtp } from '@/lib/otp';
import { verifyTurnstileToken } from '@/lib/turnstile';

/**
 * POST /api/appointments/track
 * Client Confirmed Requirement: Phone-Only Tracking with Robust Data Minimization & Ownership Verification
 *
 * Privacy & Security Invariants:
 * 1. Look up appointments by Egyptian guardian phone number.
 * 2. If queried by phone alone (without matching reference number or OTP):
 *    - STAGE 1: Returns a high-level summary list (clinic, service, date, status, guidance).
 *    - COMPLETELY REDACTS child's name ("[بيانات الطفل محجوبة لحماية الخصوصية]").
 *    - Masks reference number ("BSCH-****") and omits queue number.
 *    - Never exposes National ID, address, age, physician internal notes, or database UUIDs.
 * 3. If caller provides matching reference number or valid OTP:
 *    - STAGE 2: Unlocks full ticket receipt (masked name "أحمد ك*** م***", reference number, queue number, cancellation).
 * 4. Bot & Abuse Mitigation:
 *    - Cloudflare Turnstile bot verification (fail-secure in production).
 *    - Persistent IP rate limiting (15 requests / 10 minutes).
 *    - Persistent Phone rate limiting (8 requests / 10 minutes per phone).
 * 5. Uniform behavior: Returns empty list gracefully if no active bookings exist.
 */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const startTime = Date.now();
  try {
    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const phone = (body.phone || body.phoneNumber || '').toString().trim();
    const referenceNumber = (body.referenceNumber || body.ref || '').toString().trim().toUpperCase();
    const otpCode = (body.otp || body.code || '').toString().trim();
    const turnstileToken = body.turnstileToken || body.token || '';

    // 1. Validate Egyptian phone format
    const phoneCheck = normalizeAndValidateEgyptianPhone(phone);
    if (!phoneCheck.isValid) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'رقم الهاتف غير صحيح، يجب أن يتكون من 11 رقماً ويبدأ بـ 01 (010 / 011 / 012 / 015)' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const normalizedPhone = phoneCheck.normalized;
    const clientIp = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0].trim() || req.headers.get('x-real-ip') || '127.0.0.1';

    // 2. IP Rate Limiting
    const ipCheck = await checkIpRateLimit(clientIp);
    if (!ipCheck.allowed) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى للمحاولات من عنوان شبكتك. يرجى الانتظار والمحاولة لاحقاً.' },
        { status: 429 }
      );
    }

    // 3. Phone Rate Limiting (Persistent counter per phone)
    const phoneRateCheck = await checkRateLimit(`track_phone_${normalizedPhone}`, 8, 600);
    if (!phoneRateCheck.allowed) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى للاستعلام لهذا الرقم. يرجى الانتظار 10 دقائق قبل إعادة المحاولة.' },
        { status: 429 }
      );
    }

    // 4. Cloudflare Turnstile Bot Protection (Fails securely in production)
    const turnstileCheck = await verifyTurnstileToken(turnstileToken, clientIp);
    if (!turnstileCheck.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: turnstileCheck.error || 'فشل التحقق الأمني من مكافحة الروبوتات.' },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    // 5. Query active, upcoming appointments (Cairo timezone: date >= today, status != 'CANCELLED')
    const appointments = await appointmentStore.trackAppointmentsByVerifiedPhone(normalizedPhone);

    // Constant-time mitigation against enumeration timing attacks:
    // Ensure all requests take at least 300ms
    const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
    if (elapsed < 300) {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 300 - elapsed));
    }

    if (appointments.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        count: 0,
        appointments: [],
        message: 'لا توجد مواعيد نشطة قادمة مسجلة لهذا الرقم. يرجى التأكد من الرقم أو حجز موعد جديد.',
      });
    }

    // 5.1 Verification Check: Does the caller have proof of ownership?
    // Option A: Reference number matches an active appointment for this phone
    // Option B: Valid OTP code verified for this phone
    let isCallerVerified = false;
    if (referenceNumber) {
      isCallerVerified = appointments.some(
        (a) => a.referenceNumber.toUpperCase() === referenceNumber
      );
    }
    if (!isCallerVerified && otpCode) {
      const otpCheck = await verifyOtp(normalizedPhone, otpCode);
      if (otpCheck.valid) {
        isCallerVerified = true;
      }
    }

    // 6. Strict Data Minimization on response payload
    // If NOT verified by referenceNumber or OTP:
    // Redact child's name COMPLETELY, mask referenceNumber, omit queue number!
    const sanitizedAppointments = appointments.map((a) => {
      const isThisVerified = isCallerVerified && (!referenceNumber || a.referenceNumber.toUpperCase() === referenceNumber);

      return {
        id: isThisVerified ? a.id : undefined,
        referenceNumber: isThisVerified ? a.referenceNumber : 'BSCH-****',
        clinicId: a.clinicId,
        clinicName: a.clinicName,
        serviceId: a.serviceId,
        serviceName: a.serviceName || 'كشف واستشارة طبية',
        patientName: isThisVerified ? maskPatientName(a.patientName) : '[بيانات الطفل محجوبة لحماية الخصوصية]',
        appointmentDate: a.appointmentDate,
        attendanceGuidance: 'الحضور صباحاً بأسبقية التسجيل في شباك الاستقبال بالمستشفى',
        status: a.status,
        queueNumber: isThisVerified ? a.queueNumber : undefined,
        requiredDocuments: a.requiredDocuments || [],
        serviceInstructions: a.serviceInstructions || '',
        isVerified: isThisVerified,
      };
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      count: sanitizedAppointments.length,
      isVerified: isCallerVerified,
      appointments: sanitizedAppointments,
      message: isCallerVerified
        ? undefined
        : 'تم عرض ملخص المواعيد. للاطلاع على كامل تفاصيل التذكرة ورقم الدور أو إلغاء الحجز، يرجى إدخال الرقم المرجعي للحجز المسجل لديكم.',
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error in /api/appointments/track POST handler:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'حدث خطأ أثناء معالجة الطلب، يرجى المحاولة لاحقاً.' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

/**
 * GET /api/appointments/track
 * Directs users to the secure POST form.
 */
export async function GET() {
  return NextResponse.json({
    mode: 'PHONE_ONLY',
    message: 'خدمة متابعة الحجز متاحة عبر إدخال رقم الهاتف المسجل بالحجز.',
    privacyNotice: 'لحماية خصوصية المرضى، يتم حجب تفاصيل الاسم والبيانات الشخصية ويعرض النظام بيانات الموعد الأساسية فقط.',
  });
}


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\app\api\appointments\walk-in\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { appointmentStore } from '@/services/appointmentService';
import { AuthService } from '@/services/authService';
import { formatLocalDate } from '@/lib/rules';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    const staffUser = await AuthService.verifySession(token);

    if (!staffUser || (staffUser.role !== 'RECEPTIONIST' && staffUser.role !== 'ADMIN')) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'غير مصرح لك بإجراء حجز حضوري سريع. يرجى تسجيل الدخول بحساب استقبال أو مدير.' },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const body = await req.json();
    const { clinicId, patientName, phoneNumber, patientAge, patientAddress, appointmentDate, appointmentTime } = body;

    if (!clinicId || !patientName || !phoneNumber) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'حقول العيادة، اسم المريض، ورقم الهاتف إلزامية للحجز الحضوري' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    // Strict Clinic Authorization: Receptionist assigned to a clinic cannot book in other clinics
    if (staffUser.role === 'RECEPTIONIST' && staffUser.clinicId && staffUser.clinicId !== clinicId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'موظف الاستقبال غير مخول بالحجز لعيادة أخرى غير العيادة المخصصة له.' },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    const todayStr = formatLocalDate();
    const targetDate = appointmentDate || todayStr;

    const result = await appointmentStore.createAppointment({
      clinicId,
      patientName,
      phoneNumber,
      patientAge,
      patientAddress,
      appointmentDate: targetDate,
      appointmentTime: appointmentTime || '09:00',
      isWalkIn: true,
      staffUserId: staffUser.id,
    });

    if (!result.success || !result.appointment) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: result.error || 'تعذر تسجيل الحجز الحضوري' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: `تم تسجيل الحجز الحضوري بنجاح برقم دور #${result.appointment.queueNumber} ورقم مرجعي ${result.appointment.referenceNumber}`,
      appointment: result.appointment,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error in walk-in booking API:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حدث خطأ في الخادم' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\app\api\appointments\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { appointmentStore } from '@/services/appointmentService';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { clinicId, patientName, phoneNumber, patientAge, patientAddress, appointmentDate, appointmentTime } = body;

    if (!clinicId || !patientName || !phoneNumber || !appointmentDate) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'جميع الحقول المطلوبة (العيادة، اسم المريض، رقم الهاتف، والتاريخ) يجب ملؤها.' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const result = await appointmentStore.createAppointment({
      clinicId,
      patientName,
      phoneNumber,
      patientAge,
      patientAddress,
      appointmentDate,
      appointmentTime,
    });

    if (!result.success || !result.appointment) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: result.error || 'تعذر تسجيل الحجز، يرجى المحاولة لاحقاً.' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { appointmentTime: _removedTime, ...restAppointment } = result.appointment;
    const sanitizedAppointment = {
      ...restAppointment,
      attendanceGuidance: 'الحضور صباحاً بأسبقية التسجيل في شباك الاستقبال بالمستشفى',
    };

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: true,
        message: 'تم تسجيل طلب الحجز بنجاح',
        appointment: sanitizedAppointment,
      },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating appointment:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حدث خطأ في الخادم أثناء معالجة الطلب' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\app\api\auth\login\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { AuthService } from '@/services/authService';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { username, password } = await req.json();

    if (!username || !password) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'اسم المستخدم وكلمة المرور مطلوبان' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const session = await AuthService.validateCredentials(username, password);
    if (!session) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'بيانات الدخول غير صحيحة، يرجى التأكد من اسم المستخدم وكلمة المرور' },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'تم تسجيل الدخول بنجاح',
      user: session.user,
      token: session.token,
      expiresAt: session.expiresAt,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error during staff login:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حدث خطأ في الخادم أثناء تسجيل الدخول' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\app\api\auth\logout\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { AuthService } from '@/services/authService';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    if (token) {
      await AuthService.revokeSession(token);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Logout error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  }
}


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\app\api\auth\session\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { AuthService } from '@/services/authService';

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    const user = await AuthService.verifySession(token);

    if (!user) {
      return NextResponse.json({ authenticated: false }, { status: 401 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ authenticated: true, user });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Session verify error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ authenticated: false }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { appointmentStore } from '@/services/appointmentService';

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const searchParams = req.nextUrl.searchParams;
    const clinicId = searchParams.get('clinicId');
    const date = searchParams.get('date');

    if (!clinicId || !date) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'معرف العيادة والتاريخ مطلوبان' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const slots = appointmentStore.getAvailableSlots(clinicId, date);
    return NextResponse.json({ slots });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching slots:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'تعذر جلب المواعيد المتاحة' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\app\api\staff\appointments\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { appointmentStore } from '@/services/appointmentService';
import { AuthService } from '@/services/authService';

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    const staffUser = await AuthService.verifySession(token);

    if (!staffUser) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'غير مصرح بالوصول إلى لوحة الموظفين. يرجى تسجيل الدخول أولاً.' },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const searchParams = req.nextUrl.searchParams;
    let clinicId = searchParams.get('clinicId') || undefined;
    const status = searchParams.get('status') || undefined;
    const date = searchParams.get('date') || undefined;
    const search = searchParams.get('search') || undefined;

    // If staff user is a doctor assigned to a specific clinic, constrain to their clinic
    if (staffUser.role === 'DOCTOR' && staffUser.clinicId) {
      clinicId = staffUser.clinicId;
    }

    const appointments = await appointmentStore.getStaffAppointments(
      {
        clinicId,
        status,
        date,
        search,
      },
      staffUser
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      user: {
        id: staffUser.id,
        name: staffUser.name,
        role: staffUser.role,
        clinicId: staffUser.clinicId,
      },
      appointments,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching staff appointments:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'تعذر جلب حجوزات الموظفين' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { appointmentStore } from '@/services/appointmentService';
import { AuthService } from '@/services/authService';

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    const staffUser = await AuthService.verifySession(token);

    if (!staffUser || staffUser.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'غير مصرح بالوصول إلى إدارة العيادات. هذه الصفحة مخصصة لمدير النظام فقط.' },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    const clinics = appointmentStore.getAllClinicsIncludingInactive();
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, clinics });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching admin clinics:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حدث خطأ في الخادم' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    const staffUser = await AuthService.verifySession(token);

    if (!staffUser || staffUser.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'غير مصرح بإنشاء عيادات جديدة. هذه الصلاحية مخصصة لمدير النظام فقط.' },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    const body = await req.json();
    const result = await appointmentStore.createClinic(body, staffUser);

    if (!result.success || !result.clinic) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: result.error || 'تعذر إنشاء العيادة' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: `تم إنشاء عيادة ${result.clinic.name} بنجاح`,
      clinic: result.clinic,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating clinic:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حدث خطأ في الخادم' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { cmsService } from '@/services/cmsService';
import { AuthService } from '@/services/authService';
import { createAdminClient, isSupabaseConfigured } from '@/lib/supabase/admin';
import type { MediaAsset } from '@/types/cms';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs/promises';

const ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES = [
  'image/png',
  'image/jpeg',
  'image/jpg',
  'image/webp',
  'image/svg+xml',
  'image/x-icon',
  'image/vnd.microsoft.icon',
];
const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024; // 5MB

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    const staffUser = await AuthService.verifySession(token);

    if (!staffUser || staffUser.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'غير مصرح بعرض مكتبة الوسائط.' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const data = await cmsService.getCmsDashboardData();
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, assets: data.assets });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error fetching media assets:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حدث خطأ أثناء جلب الوسائط' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    const staffUser = await AuthService.verifySession(token);

    if (!staffUser || staffUser.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'غير مصرح برفع الوسائط. صلاحية مدير النظام مطلوبة.' },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    const contentType = req.headers.get('content-type') || '';

    // Case 1: Register existing asset via JSON
    if (contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      const body = await req.json();
      if (!body.url || !body.name) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'اسم الصورة ورابطها مطلوبان' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const asset: MediaAsset = {
        id: `custom_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`,
        name: body.name.trim(),
        url: body.url.trim(),
        mime_type: body.mime_type || 'image/png',
        size_bytes: body.size_bytes || 0,
        alt_text: body.alt_text?.trim() || body.name.trim(),
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      };

      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
        try {
          const supabase = createAdminClient();
          await (supabase.from('media_assets') as any).upsert({
            id: asset.id,
            name: asset.name,
            url: asset.url,
            mime_type: asset.mime_type,
            size_bytes: asset.size_bytes || 0,
            alt_text: asset.alt_text || null,
            storage_path: null,
            bucket_id: null,
            is_custom: true,
            created_at: asset.created_at,
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
            created_by: staffUser.username || staffUser.name || 'admin',
          });
        } catch (dbErr) {
          console.warn('Failed to persist registered media asset in Supabase:', dbErr);
        }
      }

      cmsService.registerCustomAsset(asset);
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, asset, message: 'تم تسجيل الصورة في المكتبة بنجاح' });
    }

    // Case 2: File Upload via multipart/form-data
    if (!contentType.includes('multipart/form-data')) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'نوع الطلب غير مدعوم' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const formData = await req.formData();
    const file = formData.get('file') as File | null;
    const customName = (formData.get('name') as string | null)?.trim();
    const customAlt = (formData.get('alt_text') as string | null)?.trim();

    if (!file) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'لم يتم إرسال أي ملف' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حجم الملف يتجاوز الحد المسموح به (5 ميجابايت)' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (!ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES.includes(file.type)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'صيغة الملف غير مدعومة. الصيغ المسموحة: PNG, JPEG, WebP, SVG, ICO' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'png';
    const safeBaseName = file.name
      .replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')
      .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_')
      .substring(0, 30);
    const uniqueFilename = `${Date.now()}_${safeBaseName}.${ext}`;

    const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
    let publicUrl = '';

    // Attempt Supabase storage first if configured
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = createAdminClient();
        const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('public-cms-assets')
          .upload(`uploads/${uniqueFilename}`, buffer, {
            contentType: file.type,
            upsert: false,
          });

        if (!uploadError) {
          const { data: urlData } = supabase.storage
            .from('public-cms-assets')
            .getPublicUrl(`uploads/${uniqueFilename}`);
          publicUrl = urlData.publicUrl;
        } else {
          console.warn('Supabase storage upload failed, saving to local disk:', uploadError.message);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Supabase storage exception, falling back to local:', err);
      }
    }

    // Fallback: save to public/uploads/cms locally
    if (!publicUrl) {
      const uploadDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'public', 'uploads', 'cms');
      await fs.mkdir(uploadDir, { recursive: true });
      const localFilePath = path.join(uploadDir, uniqueFilename);
      await fs.writeFile(localFilePath, buffer);
      publicUrl = `/uploads/cms/${uniqueFilename}`;
    }

    const asset: MediaAsset = {
      id: `asset_${Date.now()}`,
      name: customName || file.name,
      url: publicUrl,
      mime_type: file.type,
      size_bytes: file.size,
      alt_text: customAlt || customName || file.name,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = createAdminClient();
        await (supabase.from('media_assets') as any).upsert({
          id: asset.id,
          name: asset.name,
          url: asset.url,
          mime_type: asset.mime_type,
          size_bytes: asset.size_bytes || 0,
          alt_text: asset.alt_text || null,
          storage_path: `uploads/${uniqueFilename}`,
          bucket_id: publicUrl.includes('public-cms-assets') ? 'public-cms-assets' : null,
          is_custom: true,
          created_at: asset.created_at,
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          created_by: staffUser.username || staffUser.name || 'admin',
        });
      } catch (dbErr) {
        console.warn('Failed to persist uploaded media asset in Supabase:', dbErr);
      }
    }

    cmsService.registerCustomAsset(asset);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'تم رفع الصورة وإضافتها إلى مكتبة الوسائط بنجاح',
      asset,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error handling media upload:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حدث خطأ في الخادم أثناء رفع الملف' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}



===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\app\api\staff\cms\navigation\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { cmsService } from '@/services/cmsService';
import { AuthService } from '@/services/authService';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    const staffUser = await AuthService.verifySession(token);

    if (!staffUser || staffUser.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'غير مصرح بتعديل روابط القوائم. صلاحية مدير النظام مطلوبة.' },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    const body = await req.json();
    const items = Array.isArray(body) ? body : body.items;

    if (!Array.isArray(items)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'صيغة بيانات عناصر القائمة غير صحيحة' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const result = await cmsService.updateNavigation(items, staffUser);
    if (!result.success || !result.navigation) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: result.error || 'فشل في تحديث عناصر القوائم' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'تم حفظ عناصر التنقل ونشرها بنجاح',
      navigation: result.navigation,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error updating navigation items:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حدث خطأ في الخادم أثناء تحديث عناصر القوائم' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}



===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\app\api\staff\cms\sections\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { cmsService } from '@/services/cmsService';
import { AuthService } from '@/services/authService';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();
    const staffUser = await AuthService.verifySession(token);

    if (!staffUser || staffUser.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'غير مصرح بتعديل أقسام الصفحة الرئيسية. صلاحية مدير النظام مطلوبة.' },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    const body = await req.json();

    if (body.action === 'reorder') {
      if (!Array.isArray(body.sections)) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'قائمة ترتيب الأقسام غير صحيحة' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const result = await cmsService.reorderSections(body.sections, staffUser);
      if (!result.success) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: result.error || 'فشل في إعادة ترتيب الأقسام' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        message: 'تم تحديث ترتيب الأقسام ونشره بنجاح',
        sections: result.sections,
      });
    }

    const { sectionId, updates } = body;
    if (!sectionId || !updates) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'معرّف القسم والبيانات المطلوبة للتعديل ناقصة' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const result = await cmsService.updateSection(sectionId, updates, staffUser);
    if (!result.success || !result.section) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: result.error || 'فشل في تحديث بيانات القسم' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: `تم تحديث قسم (${result.section.title || sectionId}) ونشره بنجاح`,
      section: result.section,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error updating CMS section:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'حدث خطأ في الخادم أثناء تحديث القسم' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

}

===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\lib\supabase\admin.ts =====
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database } from '../../types/database.types.ts';

/**
 * Creates an administrative Supabase client using the server-side Service Role Key.
 *
 * Security Invariants:
 * 1. NEVER falls back to NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY or Publishable key.
 * 2. In production (NODE_ENV === 'production'), strictly fails closed by throwing an Error
 *    if the Service Role key is unconfigured, preventing silent privilege degradation.
 * 3. Never exposed or shipped to the browser or client components.
 */
export function createAdminClient() {
  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const supabaseServiceRoleKey =
    process.env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY ||
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;

  if (!supabaseUrl || !supabaseServiceRoleKey) {
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
      throw new Error(
        'CRITICAL: Supabase admin client cannot be created in production without valid service role key (SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY or SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY).'
      );
    }
    // Safe offline placeholder strictly for local development and unit test harness
    return createClient<Database>(
      supabaseUrl || 'https://placeholder-project.supabase.co',
      'placeholder-service-role-key',
      {
        auth: {
          autoRefreshToken: false,
          persistSession: false,
        },
      }
    );
  }

  return createClient<Database>(supabaseUrl, supabaseServiceRoleKey, {
    auth: {
      autoRefreshToken: false,
      persistSession: false,
    },
  });
}

/**
 * Creates an authenticated Supabase client scoped to a verified user JWT.
 *
 * Security Invariants:
 * 1. Attaches the user's verified Bearer JWT to all outgoing requests.
 * 2. Executes in PostgreSQL under the `authenticated` role with `auth.uid()` set to the user ID.
 * 3. Enforces PostgreSQL Row Level Security (RLS) policies and triggers natively.
 */
export function createUserClient(userJwtToken: string) {
  if (!userJwtToken || !userJwtToken.trim()) {
    throw new Error('User JWT token is required to create an authenticated Supabase user client.');
  }

  const cleanToken = userJwtToken.trim();
  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || 'https://placeholder-project.supabase.co';
  const supabaseAnonKey =
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY ||
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ||
    'placeholder-anon-key';

  return createClient<Database>(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey, {
    global: {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${cleanToken}`,
      },
    },
    auth: {
      autoRefreshToken: false,
      persistSession: false,
    },
  });
}

export function isSupabaseConfigured(): boolean {
  const url = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const anonKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
  return Boolean(
    url &&
    !url.includes('placeholder') &&
    anonKey &&
    !anonKey.includes('placeholder')
  );
}

/**
 * Validates if Supabase server-side service-role key is provided for administrative writes.
 */
export function isSupabaseAdminConfigured(): boolean {
  const url = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const serviceKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY || process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  return Boolean(
    url &&
    !url.includes('placeholder') &&
    serviceKey &&
    !serviceKey.includes('placeholder')
  );
}

/**
 * Determines whether in-memory mock fallback operations are permitted.
 * Rules:
 * 1. If Supabase is configured, all operations MUST target Supabase; mock fallback is FORBIDDEN.
 * 2. In production (NODE_ENV === 'production'), mock fallback is STRICTLY FORBIDDEN.
 * 3. In development/testing, mock fallback is only allowed when Supabase is explicitly unconfigured.
 */
export function isMockStoreAllowed(): boolean {
  if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    return false;
  }
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\lib\supabase\server.ts =====
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { Database } from '@/types/database.types';

export async function createClient() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || 'https://placeholder-project.supabase.co';
  const supabaseAnonKey =
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY ||
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ||
    'placeholder-anon-key';

  return createServerClient<Database>(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey, {
    cookies: {
      get(name: string) {
        return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
      },
      set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
        try {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        } catch {
          // The `set` method was called from a Server Component.
          // This can be ignored if you have middleware refreshing user sessions.
        }
      },
      remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
        try {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options, maxAge: 0 });
        } catch {
          // The `remove` method was called from a Server Component.
        }
      },
    },
  });
}


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\lib\otp.test.ts =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { generateOtp, verifyOtp, _resetOtpStoreForTesting, OTP_CONFIG, hashOtp, constantTimeHashCompare, checkIpRateLimit } from './otp.ts';
import { appointmentStore } from '../services/appointmentService.ts';

test('OTP Invariant 1: generateOtp creates cryptographically valid 6-digit code and enforces 5-min expiry', async () => {
  _resetOtpStoreForTesting();
  const phone = '01011112222';
  const result = await generateOtp(phone);

  assert.equal(result.success, true);
  assert.equal(OTP_CONFIG.CODE_LENGTH, 6, 'OTP length must be 6 digits');
  assert.ok(result.expiresAt && result.expiresAt > Date.now(), 'Expiry must be in future');
  assert.ok(result.expiresAt && result.expiresAt <= Date.now() + OTP_CONFIG.EXPIRY_MS, 'Expiry must be within 5 mins');
});

test('OTP Invariant 2: verifyOtp accepts correct 6-digit code, rejects wrong code, and prevents replay attacks', async () => {
  _resetOtpStoreForTesting();
  const phone = '01022223333';
  await generateOtp(phone);

  // In test mode, verify wrong 6-digit code first
  const wrongRes = await verifyOtp(phone, '000000');
  assert.equal(wrongRes.valid, false, 'Wrong code must be rejected');
  assert.ok(wrongRes.attemptsRemaining !== undefined && wrongRes.attemptsRemaining < 5);

  // When NODE_ENV === 'development', 123456 is accepted as fallback
  const originalEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;
  try {
    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = 'development';
    const validRes = await verifyOtp(phone, '123456');
    assert.equal(validRes.valid, true, 'Valid code must be accepted in development');

    // Replay attempt must be rejected because code is consumed upon success
    const replayRes = await verifyOtp(phone, '123456');
    assert.equal(replayRes.valid, false, 'Replay must be strictly rejected');
  } finally {
    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = originalEnv;
  }
});

test('OTP Invariant 3: Request Rate Limiting enforces 60-second cooldown between requests', async () => {
  _resetOtpStoreForTesting();
  const phone = '01033334444';

  const res1 = await generateOtp(phone);
  assert.equal(res1.success, true, 'First request must succeed');

  // Immediate second request must be rate-limited
  const res2 = await generateOtp(phone);
  assert.equal(res2.success, false, 'Immediate second request must be blocked');
  assert.ok(res2.cooldownSeconds && res2.cooldownSeconds > 0, 'Cooldown seconds must be returned');
  assert.match(res2.error || '', /يرجى الانتظار/, 'Cooldown error message must be returned');
});

test('OTP Invariant 4: Brute Force Protection locks out and deletes code after 5 failed attempts', async () => {
  _resetOtpStoreForTesting();
  const phone = '01044445555';
  await generateOtp(phone);

  // 5 consecutive wrong attempts
  for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
    const res = await verifyOtp(phone, `99900${i}`);
    assert.equal(res.valid, false);
    assert.equal(res.attemptsRemaining, 5 - i);
  }

  // 5th wrong attempt should lock out and delete entry
  const fifthRes = await verifyOtp(phone, '999005');
  assert.equal(fifthRes.valid, false);
  assert.match(fifthRes.error || '', /تجاوز الحد الأقصى/);

  // 6th attempt should report expired/deleted
  const sixthRes = await verifyOtp(phone, '123456');
  assert.equal(sixthRes.valid, false);
});

test('OTP Invariant 5: Production Security - Dev bypass code 123456 is strictly forbidden outside development', async () => {
  _resetOtpStoreForTesting();
  const phone = '01055556666';

  // Generate real OTP
  const genRes = await generateOtp(phone);
  assert.equal(genRes.success, true);

  const originalEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;
  try {
    // 1. In production
    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = 'production';
    const prodRes = await verifyOtp(phone, '123456');
    assert.equal(prodRes.valid, false, 'Dev bypass 123456 must be strictly rejected in production');

    // 2. In staging / test / undefined (anything other than exact 'development')
    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = 'test';
    const testRes = await verifyOtp(phone, '123456');
    assert.equal(testRes.valid, false, 'Dev bypass 123456 must be strictly rejected in test environment');
  } finally {
    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = originalEnv;
  }
});

test('OTP Invariant 6: Production Security - demoNotice is never returned outside development', async () => {
  _resetOtpStoreForTesting();
  const phone = '01077778888';
  const originalEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;

  try {
    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = 'production';
    const prodGen = await generateOtp(phone);
    assert.equal(prodGen.demoNotice, undefined, 'demoNotice must be strictly undefined in production');

    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = 'development';
    _resetOtpStoreForTesting();
    const devGen = await generateOtp(phone);
    assert.ok(typeof devGen.demoNotice === 'string', 'demoNotice is provided only in development');
  } finally {
    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = originalEnv;
  }
});

test('OTP Invariant 7: Cumulative Brute-Force Counter is Retained Across Re-Requests Within Hourly Window', async () => {
  _resetOtpStoreForTesting();
  const phone = '01088889999';

  // Request code #1
  await generateOtp(phone);

  // Make 3 failed attempts
  await verifyOtp(phone, '111111');
  await verifyOtp(phone, '222222');
  const res3 = await verifyOtp(phone, '333333');
  assert.equal(res3.attemptsRemaining, 2, 'Should have 2 attempts remaining');

  // Fast-forward cooldown (simulate wait) by adjusting lastRequestedAt in store
  const originalEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;
  try {
    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = 'development';
    // Generate code #2 after cooldown
    // Verify that the new code DOES NOT reset attempts counter back to 0
    const store = (globalThis as any).otpStoreInstance as Map<string, any>;
    if (store && store.has(phone)) {
      const entry = store.get(phone);
      entry.lastRequestedAt = Date.now() - 70 * 1000; // passed 60s cooldown
    }

    const reReq = await generateOtp(phone);
    assert.equal(reReq.success, true);

    // Make 2 more wrong attempts with new code: should reach 5 total and lockout!
    await verifyOtp(phone, '444444');
    const fifth = await verifyOtp(phone, '555555');
    assert.equal(fifth.valid, false);
    assert.match(fifth.error || '', /تجاوز الحد الأقصى/);
  } finally {
    (process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = originalEnv;
  }
});

test('OTP Invariant 8: Keyed HMAC-SHA256 and constant-time comparison protect against timing attacks', () => {
  const phone = '01012345678';
  const code = '654321';
  const hmac1 = hashOtp(phone, code);
  const hmac2 = hashOtp(phone, code);
  const hmacOther = hashOtp(phone, '654322');

  assert.equal(hmac1, hmac2, 'HMAC must be deterministic for same phone & code');
  assert.notEqual(hmac1, hmacOther, 'Different codes must produce completely different HMAC digests');
  assert.equal(constantTimeHashCompare(hmac1, hmac2), true);
  assert.equal(constantTimeHashCompare(hmac1, hmacOther), false);
  assert.equal(constantTimeHashCompare(hmac1, 'invalid'), false);
});

test('OTP Invariant 9: IP Rate Limiting blocks repeated abusive requests from same IP', async () => {
  _resetOtpStoreForTesting();
  const abusiveIp = '198.51.100.42';

  // First 15 requests should be allowed
  for (let i = 0; i < OTP_CONFIG.MAX_IP_REQUESTS; i++) {
    const check = await checkIpRateLimit(abusiveIp);
    assert.equal(check.allowed, true, `Request ${i + 1} should be allowed`);
  }

  // 16th request must be blocked
  const blockedCheck = await checkIpRateLimit(abusiveIp);
  assert.equal(blockedCheck.allowed, false, '16th request from same IP must be rate-limited');
  assert.ok(blockedCheck.retryAfterSeconds && blockedCheck.retryAfterSeconds > 0);
});

test('Tracking Invariant 10: Phone tracking returns only sanitized fields (no sensitive PII leaked)', async () => {
  const verifiedPhone = '01012345678';
  const appointments = await appointmentStore.trackAppointmentsByVerifiedPhone(verifiedPhone);

  assert.ok(appointments.length > 0, 'Should find appointments for test fixture');

  for (const apt of appointments) {
    assert.ok(apt.referenceNumber, 'referenceNumber must exist');
    assert.ok(apt.patientName, 'patientName must exist');
    assert.ok(apt.clinicName, 'clinicName must exist');
    assert.ok(apt.appointmentDate, 'appointmentDate must exist');

    const rawKeys = Object.keys(apt);
    assert.ok(!rawKeys.includes('nationalId'), 'National ID must not be present in public tracking');
    assert.ok(!rawKeys.includes('guardianNationalId'), 'Guardian National ID must not be present in public tracking');
    assert.ok(!rawKeys.includes('medicalHistory'), 'Medical history notes must not be present in public tracking');
  }
});


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\lib\otp.ts =====
/**
 * BSCH Clinics - Secure Persistent OTP Verification Service for Phone-Only Tracking
 *
 * Security Invariants & Compliance (Hardened):
 * 1. Cryptographically secure random 6-digit code generation using Node.js crypto CSPRNG (100000 - 999999).
 * 2. Persistent storage in Supabase `public.otp_verifications` across serverless instances.
 * 3. Keyed HMAC-SHA256 Hashing: Only the HMAC digest is stored in the database; plaintext codes are never persisted.
 * 4. Timing-attack protection via `crypto.timingSafeEqual` for all hash comparisons.
 * 5. Strict 5-minute server-side expiration.
 * 6. Replay prevention: OTP record is deleted immediately upon successful verification.
 * 7. Brute-force lockout: Max 5 incorrect attempts before code is invalidated, retained across re-requests within the hourly window.
 * 8. Request Rate Limiting: 60-second cooldown per phone, max 5 requests/hour per phone, and per-IP rate limiting.
 * 9. Zero-logging: Plaintext OTP codes and HMACs are never logged.
 * 10. Zero production exposure: Plaintext codes and demoNotice are strictly undefined outside development.
 * 11. Strict dev bypass: Static test code ('123456') is allowed ONLY when `process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'` (explicit allowlist).
 * 12. Offline Fallback: Controlled in-memory fallback ONLY in dev/testing when Supabase is explicitly unconfigured.
 */

import { randomInt, createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto';
import { createAdminClient, isSupabaseConfigured, isMockStoreAllowed } from './supabase/admin.ts';

export interface OtpEntry {
  codeHash: string;
  expiresAt: number;
  attempts: number;
  lastRequestedAt: number;
  hourlyRequestCount: number;
  windowStart: number;
}

// In-memory fallback map strictly for offline development and local unit tests
const globalForOtp = globalThis as unknown as {
  otpStoreInstance?: Map<string, OtpEntry>;
  ipRateLimitStoreInstance?: Map<string, { count: number; resetAt: number }>;
};

const memoryOtpStore = globalForOtp.otpStoreInstance ?? new Map<string, OtpEntry>();
const memoryIpStore = globalForOtp.ipRateLimitStoreInstance ?? new Map<string, { count: number; resetAt: number }>();

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
  globalForOtp.otpStoreInstance = memoryOtpStore;
  globalForOtp.ipRateLimitStoreInstance = memoryIpStore;
}

// Security Configuration
export const OTP_CONFIG = {
  CODE_LENGTH: 6,
  EXPIRY_MS: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes
  MAX_ATTEMPTS: 5,
  REQUEST_COOLDOWN_MS: 60 * 1000, // 60 seconds between requests
  MAX_HOURLY_REQUESTS: 5,
  HOURLY_WINDOW_MS: 60 * 60 * 1000, // 1 hour
  DEV_BYPASS_CODE: '123456',
  IP_WINDOW_MS: 10 * 60 * 1000, // 10 minutes
  MAX_IP_REQUESTS: 15,
};

export interface GenerateOtpResult {
  success: boolean;
  expiresAt?: number;
  cooldownSeconds?: number;
  error?: string;
  demoNotice?: string;
}

export interface VerifyOtpResult {
  valid: boolean;
  error?: string;
  attemptsRemaining?: number;
}

/**
 * Generate a keyed HMAC-SHA256 digest of the phone and OTP code using OTP_HASH_SECRET.
 */
export function hashOtp(phone: string, code: string): string {
  const secret =
    process.env.OTP_HASH_SECRET ||
    process.env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY ||
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ||
    'bsch_otp_hmac_secret_pediatric_hospital_2026';

  return createHmac('sha256', secret)
    .update(`${phone.trim()}:${code.trim()}`)
    .digest('hex');
}

/**
 * Timing-safe string comparison to prevent side-channel timing attacks.
 */
export function constantTimeHashCompare(hashA: string, hashB: string): boolean {
  if (!hashA || !hashB) return false;
  const bufA = Buffer.from(hashA, 'hex');
  const bufB = Buffer.from(hashB, 'hex');
  if (bufA.length !== bufB.length) return false;
  return timingSafeEqual(bufA, bufB);
}

/**
 * Generic persistent rate limiter helper with serverless fallback:
 * - Checks persistent PostgreSQL atomic rate limit counter via service_role RPC if Supabase is configured.
 * - Falls back to local memory store for offline development and unit tests.
 */
export async function checkRateLimit(
  key: string,
  maxRequests: number,
  windowSeconds: number
): Promise<{ allowed: boolean; retryAfterSeconds?: number }> {
  const cleanKey = key.trim();
  const isProd = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';

  // 1. If Supabase is configured, use atomic persistent rate limiting table
  if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    try {
      const supabase = createAdminClient();
      const { data, error } = await (supabase as any).rpc('check_rate_limit', {
        p_key: cleanKey,
        p_max_requests: maxRequests,
        p_window_seconds: windowSeconds,
      });

      if (!error && data && typeof data === 'object') {
        const res = data as { allowed: boolean; retry_after_seconds?: number };
        return {
          allowed: res.allowed,
          retryAfterSeconds: res.retry_after_seconds,
        };
      }

      if (isProd) {
        console.error('CRITICAL: Persistent rate limiting RPC failed in production:', error);
        return { allowed: false, retryAfterSeconds: 60 };
      }
    } catch (rpcErr) {
      if (isProd) {
        console.error('CRITICAL: Persistent rate limit network/client failure in production:', rpcErr);
        return { allowed: false, retryAfterSeconds: 60 };
      }
      console.warn('Persistent rate limit RPC fallback to memory:', rpcErr);
    }
  } else if (isProd) {
    console.error('CRITICAL: Rate limiting is unconfigured in production (persistent Supabase store required).');
    return { allowed: false, retryAfterSeconds: 60 };
  }

  // 2. In-memory fallback (offline tests, local dev)
  const now = Date.now();
  const entry = memoryIpStore.get(cleanKey);
  const windowMs = windowSeconds * 1000;

  if (!entry || entry.resetAt < now) {
    memoryIpStore.set(cleanKey, { count: 1, resetAt: now + windowMs });
    return { allowed: true };
  }

  if (entry.count >= maxRequests) {
    const retryAfter = Math.ceil((entry.resetAt - now) / 1000);
    return { allowed: false, retryAfterSeconds: retryAfter };
  }

  entry.count++;
  return { allowed: true };
}

/**
 * Per-IP rate limiting helper:
 * - Checks persistent PostgreSQL atomic rate limit counter via service_role RPC if Supabase is configured.
 * - Falls back to local memory store for offline development and unit tests.
 */
export async function checkIpRateLimit(ip: string): Promise<{ allowed: boolean; retryAfterSeconds?: number }> {
  const cleanIp = ip.trim() || '127.0.0.1';
  // Allow localhost loopback in development without restrictions
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development' && (cleanIp === '127.0.0.1' || cleanIp === '::1' || cleanIp === 'localhost')) {
    return { allowed: true };
  }

  return checkRateLimit(
    `ip_limit_${cleanIp}`,
    OTP_CONFIG.MAX_IP_REQUESTS,
    Math.ceil(OTP_CONFIG.IP_WINDOW_MS / 1000)
  );
}

/**
 * Generate a cryptographically secure 6-digit OTP for a verified phone number.
 * Persists to Supabase `public.otp_verifications` if configured, with rate-limiting, IP limits, and expiration.
 */
export async function generateOtp(phone: string, clientIp?: string): Promise<GenerateOtpResult> {
  const cleanPhone = phone.trim();
  const now = Date.now();
  const isStrictDev = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development';

  // 0. Check IP Rate Limit
  if (clientIp) {
    const ipCheck = await checkIpRateLimit(clientIp);
    if (!ipCheck.allowed) {
      return {
        success: false,
        cooldownSeconds: ipCheck.retryAfterSeconds,
        error: `تم تجاوز معدل الطلبات من هذا العنوان. يرجى الانتظار ${ipCheck.retryAfterSeconds} ثانية.`,
      };
    }
  }

  // Generate cryptographically secure 6-digit code (100000 to 999999)
  const code = randomInt(100000, 1000000).toString();
  const codeHash = hashOtp(cleanPhone, code);
  const expiresAt = now + OTP_CONFIG.EXPIRY_MS;

  // 1. Production / Persistent Path via Supabase
  if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    try {
      const supabase = createAdminClient();

      // Check existing record for rate-limiting & cumulative attempts
      const { data: existing, error: fetchErr } = await (supabase as any)
        .from('otp_verifications')
        .select('*')
        .eq('phone', cleanPhone)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (fetchErr) {
        console.error('Supabase fetch otp_verifications error:', fetchErr.code);
      }

      let hourlyCount = 1;
      let windowStart = new Date(now).toISOString();
      let retainedAttempts = 0;

      if (existing) {
        // Enforce cooldown (60 seconds)
        const lastReq = new Date(existing.last_requested_at).getTime();
        const timeSinceLast = now - lastReq;
        if (timeSinceLast < OTP_CONFIG.REQUEST_COOLDOWN_MS) {
          const waitSec = Math.ceil((OTP_CONFIG.REQUEST_COOLDOWN_MS - timeSinceLast) / 1000);
          return {
            success: false,
            cooldownSeconds: waitSec,
            error: `يرجى الانتظار ${waitSec} ثانية قبل طلب رمز تحقق جديد.`,
          };
        }

        // Check cumulative hourly window
        const existingWinStart = new Date(existing.window_start).getTime();
        if (now - existingWinStart < OTP_CONFIG.HOURLY_WINDOW_MS) {
          // If phone is already locked out by brute-force within this hour, deny new code
          if ((existing.attempts || 0) >= OTP_CONFIG.MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
            return {
              success: false,
              error: 'تم إيقاف طلب رموز التحقق مؤقتاً لهذا الرقم بسبب استنفاد محاولات الدخول الخاطئة. يرجى المحاولة لاحقاً.',
            };
          }

          if (existing.hourly_request_count >= OTP_CONFIG.MAX_HOURLY_REQUESTS) {
            return {
              success: false,
              error: 'تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى لطلبات التحقق لهذا الرقم خلال هذه الساعة. يرجى المحاولة لاحقاً.',
            };
          }

          hourlyCount = (existing.hourly_request_count || 0) + 1;
          windowStart = existing.window_start;
          // Retain cumulative failed attempts within the window so requesting a new code doesn't reset brute-force counter
          retainedAttempts = existing.attempts || 0;
        }
      }

      // Upsert record with HMAC-SHA256 into public.otp_verifications
      const { error: upsertErr } = await (supabase as any)
        .from('otp_verifications')
        .upsert(
          {
            phone: cleanPhone,
            code_hash: codeHash,
            attempts: retainedAttempts,
            expires_at: new Date(expiresAt).toISOString(),
            last_requested_at: new Date(now).toISOString(),
            hourly_request_count: hourlyCount,
            window_start: windowStart,
          },
          { onConflict: 'phone' }
        );

      if (upsertErr) {
        console.error('Supabase upsert otp_verifications error:', upsertErr.code);
        return {
          success: false,
          error: 'تعذر حفظ رمز التحقق في قاعدة البيانات، يرجى المحاولة لاحقاً.',
        };
      }

      return {
        success: true,
        expiresAt,
        // demoNotice is defined ONLY when NODE_ENV === 'development'
        demoNotice: isStrictDev ? `لأغراض التطوير المحلي، رمز التحقق هو: ${code}` : undefined,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Supabase OTP generation exception');
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'حدث خطأ في خدمة التحقق المركزية.',
      };
    }
  }

  // 2. Fallback check: If in production without Supabase, refuse in-memory fallback
  if (!isMockStoreAllowed()) {
    return {
      success: false,
      error: 'خدمة التحقق برمز OTP غير مهيأة بقاعدة بيانات الإنتاج.',
    };
  }

  // 3. Local In-Memory Fallback for Offline Development & Unit Testing
  const existingMem = memoryOtpStore.get(cleanPhone);
  if (existingMem) {
    const timeSinceLast = now - existingMem.lastRequestedAt;
    if (timeSinceLast < OTP_CONFIG.REQUEST_COOLDOWN_MS) {
      const waitSec = Math.ceil((OTP_CONFIG.REQUEST_COOLDOWN_MS - timeSinceLast) / 1000);
      return {
        success: false,
        cooldownSeconds: waitSec,
        error: `يرجى الانتظار ${waitSec} ثانية قبل طلب رمز تحقق جديد.`,
      };
    }

    let memHourly = existingMem.hourlyRequestCount;
    let memWinStart = existingMem.windowStart;
    let retainedAttempts = existingMem.attempts;

    if (now - memWinStart > OTP_CONFIG.HOURLY_WINDOW_MS) {
      memHourly = 0;
      memWinStart = now;
      retainedAttempts = 0;
    } else {
      if (existingMem.attempts >= OTP_CONFIG.MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: 'تم إيقاف طلب رموز التحقق مؤقتاً لهذا الرقم بسبب استنفاد محاولات الدخول الخاطئة. يرجى المحاولة لاحقاً.',
        };
      }
    }

    if (memHourly >= OTP_CONFIG.MAX_HOURLY_REQUESTS) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى لطلبات التحقق لهذا الرقم خلال هذه الساعة. يرجى المحاولة لاحقاً.',
      };
    }

    memoryOtpStore.set(cleanPhone, {
      codeHash,
      expiresAt,
      attempts: retainedAttempts,
      lastRequestedAt: now,
      hourlyRequestCount: memHourly + 1,
      windowStart: memWinStart,
    });
  } else {
    memoryOtpStore.set(cleanPhone, {
      codeHash,
      expiresAt,
      attempts: 0,
      lastRequestedAt: now,
      hourlyRequestCount: 1,
      windowStart: now,
    });
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    expiresAt,
    demoNotice: isStrictDev ? `لأغراض التطوير المحلي، رمز التحقق هو: ${code}` : undefined,
  };
}

/**
 * Verify a submitted 6-digit OTP code.
 * Enforces server-side expiration, brute-force attempt limits, and single-use replay protection.
 * Strict: dev bypass code is allowed ONLY when process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'.
 */
export async function verifyOtp(phone: string, inputCode: string): Promise<VerifyOtpResult> {
  const cleanPhone = phone.trim();
  const cleanCode = (inputCode || '').trim();
  const now = Date.now();
  const isStrictDev = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development';

  if (!cleanCode || cleanCode.length !== OTP_CONFIG.CODE_LENGTH) {
    return { valid: false, error: 'يرجى إدخال رمز التحقق المكون من 6 أرقام.' };
  }

  // 1. Production / Persistent Path via Supabase
  if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    try {
      const supabase = createAdminClient();

      const { data: entry, error: fetchErr } = await (supabase as any)
        .from('otp_verifications')
        .select('*')
        .eq('phone', cleanPhone)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (fetchErr || !entry) {
        return { valid: false, error: 'لم يتم طلب رمز تحقق لهذا الرقم أو انتهت صلاحيته.' };
      }

      // Expiration check
      const exp = new Date(entry.expires_at).getTime();
      if (exp < now) {
        await (supabase as any).from('otp_verifications').delete().eq('phone', cleanPhone);
        return { valid: false, error: 'انتهت صلاحية رمز التحقق، يرجى طلب رمز جديد.' };
      }

      // Brute-force lockout check
      if (entry.attempts >= OTP_CONFIG.MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
        await (supabase as any).from('otp_verifications').delete().eq('phone', cleanPhone);
        return { valid: false, error: 'تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى للمحاولات الخاطئة. تم إلغاء الرمز لأسباب أمنية.' };
      }

      // Timing-safe HMAC comparison
      const inputHash = hashOtp(cleanPhone, cleanCode);
      const isCodeCorrect = constantTimeHashCompare(entry.code_hash, inputHash);
      const isDevBypass = isStrictDev && cleanCode === OTP_CONFIG.DEV_BYPASS_CODE;

      if (isCodeCorrect || isDevBypass) {
        // Successful verification - immediately delete to prevent replay attacks
        await (supabase as any).from('otp_verifications').delete().eq('phone', cleanPhone);
        return { valid: true };
      }

      // Increment attempt counter in DB
      const newAttempts = (entry.attempts || 0) + 1;
      if (newAttempts >= OTP_CONFIG.MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
        await (supabase as any).from('otp_verifications').delete().eq('phone', cleanPhone);
        return { valid: false, error: 'تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى للمحاولات الخاطئة. تم إلغاء الرمز لأسباب أمنية.' };
      }

      await (supabase as any)
        .from('otp_verifications')
        .update({ attempts: newAttempts })
        .eq('phone', cleanPhone);

      const remaining = OTP_CONFIG.MAX_ATTEMPTS - newAttempts;
      return {
        valid: false,
        attemptsRemaining: remaining,
        error: `رمز التحقق غير صحيح. المحاولات المتبقية: ${remaining}`,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Supabase OTP verification exception');
      return { valid: false, error: 'حدث خطأ أثناء التحقق من الرمز.' };
    }
  }

  // 2. Fallback check: If in production without Supabase, refuse in-memory fallback
  if (!isMockStoreAllowed()) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      error: 'خدمة التحقق برمز OTP غير مهيأة بقاعدة بيانات الإنتاج.',
    };
  }

  // 3. Local In-Memory Fallback for Offline Development & Unit Testing
  const entry = memoryOtpStore.get(cleanPhone);
  if (!entry) {
    return { valid: false, error: 'لم يتم طلب رمز تحقق لهذا الرقم أو انتهت صلاحيته.' };
  }

  if (entry.expiresAt < now) {
    memoryOtpStore.delete(cleanPhone);
    return { valid: false, error: 'انتهت صلاحية رمز التحقق، يرجى طلب رمز جديد.' };
  }

  if (entry.attempts >= OTP_CONFIG.MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
    memoryOtpStore.delete(cleanPhone);
    return { valid: false, error: 'تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى للمحاولات الخاطئة. تم إلغاء الرمز لأسباب أمنية.' };
  }

  entry.attempts++;

  const inputHash = hashOtp(cleanPhone, cleanCode);
  const isCodeCorrect = constantTimeHashCompare(entry.codeHash, inputHash);
  const isDevBypass = isStrictDev && cleanCode === OTP_CONFIG.DEV_BYPASS_CODE;

  if (isCodeCorrect || isDevBypass) {
    memoryOtpStore.delete(cleanPhone);
    return { valid: true };
  }

  const remaining = OTP_CONFIG.MAX_ATTEMPTS - entry.attempts;
  if (remaining <= 0) {
    memoryOtpStore.delete(cleanPhone);
    return { valid: false, error: 'تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى للمحاولات الخاطئة. تم إلغاء الرمز لأسباب أمنية.' };
  }

  return {
    valid: false,
    attemptsRemaining: remaining,
    error: `رمز التحقق غير صحيح. المحاولات المتبقية: ${remaining}`,
  };
}

/**
 * Clear the in-memory fallback store (used for test teardowns).
 */
export function _resetOtpStoreForTesting(): void {
  memoryOtpStore.clear();
  memoryIpStore.clear();
}


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\lib\persistence.test.ts =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { appointmentStore } from '../services/appointmentService.ts';
import { cmsService } from '../services/cmsService.ts';
import type { StaffUser } from '../types/index.ts';

const adminUser: StaffUser = {
  id: 'admin-test',
  username: 'admin',
  name: 'مدير النظام التجريبي',
  role: 'ADMIN',
  active: true,
};

test('Persistence Test 1: Booking failure in Supabase NEVER returns a false success ticket', async () => {
  // Set environment to simulate configured Supabase
  const originalUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const originalKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;

  try {
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = 'http://127.0.0.1:54321';
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = 'mock-anon-key-for-test';

    // Calling createAppointment when Supabase write fails must reject cleanly
    const result = await appointmentStore.createAppointment({
      clinicId: 'dentistry',
      patientName: 'كريم أحمد محمد يوسف',
      phoneNumber: '01012345678',
      patientAge: '5 سنوات',
      patientAddress: 'أبو حمص - دمسنا - البحيرة',
      appointmentDate: '2026-11-21',
    });

    assert.equal(result.success, false, 'createAppointment must return success=false on DB failure');
    assert.equal(result.appointment, undefined, 'No appointment ticket may be issued when persistence fails');
    assert.ok(result.error && result.error.length > 0, 'Must provide clear Arabic error message to user');
    assert.match(result.error!, /قاعدة البيانات|تعذر/, 'Error must indicate persistence/connection failure');
  } finally {
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = originalUrl;
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = originalKey;
  }
});

test('Persistence Test 2: direct createAppointment rejects without falling back to memory when DB fails', async () => {
  const originalUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const originalKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;

  try {
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = 'http://127.0.0.1:54321';
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = 'mock-anon-key-for-test';

    const result = await appointmentStore.createAppointment({
      clinicId: 'echo',
      patientName: 'سارة إبراهيم علي مصطفى',
      phoneNumber: '01122334455',
      appointmentDate: '2026-11-21',
    });

    assert.equal(result.success, false, 'createAppointment must return false when Supabase fails');
    assert.equal(result.appointment, undefined, 'Must not return an unpersisted appointment object');
    assert.ok(result.error, 'Must provide error description');
  } finally {
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = originalUrl;
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = originalKey;
  }
});

      adminUser
    );

    assert.ok(result.error, 'Error message must be returned');
  } finally {
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = originalUrl;
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = originalKey;
  }

  assert.equal(
    afterContent.settings.site_name,
    initialSiteName,
    'Settings in cache must remain untouched when DB write fails'
  );
});

test('Persistence Test 5: Production mode strictly forbids in-memory fallback when Supabase is unconfigured', async () => {
  const originalNodeEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;
  const originalUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const originalKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;

  try {
    (process.env as any).NODE_ENV = 'production';
    delete process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
    delete process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;

    // Booking attempt in production without Supabase
    const bookingResult = await appointmentStore.createAppointment({
      clinicId: 'gi',
      patientName: 'عمر طارق إبراهيم حسن',
      phoneNumber: '01234567890',
      appointmentDate: '2026-11-25',
    });
    assert.equal(bookingResult.success, false, 'Production without Supabase must reject booking');
    assert.match(bookingResult.error || '', /إنتاج|قاعدة البيانات/);

    // CMS save attempt in production without Supabase
    const cmsResult = await cmsService.updateSettings({ emergency_hotline: '999' }, adminUser);
    assert.equal(cmsResult.success, false, 'Production without Supabase must reject CMS save');
    assert.match(cmsResult.error || '', /إنتاج|قاعدة البيانات/);
  } finally {
    (process.env as any).NODE_ENV = originalNodeEnv;
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = originalUrl;
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = originalKey;
  }
});

test('Supabase RLS role permission matrix validation', () => {
  // Test role permission rules matching the SQL policies
  const canAdminManageClinics = (role: string) => role === 'ADMIN';
  const canViewPatientList = (role: string) => ['ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST', 'DOCTOR'].includes(role);
  const canConfirmBookings = (role: string) => ['ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST'].includes(role);
  const canCompleteAppointment = (role: string) => ['ADMIN', 'DOCTOR'].includes(role);
  const canViewAuditLogs = (role: string) => role === 'ADMIN';

  assert.equal(canAdminManageClinics('ADMIN'), true);
  assert.equal(canAdminManageClinics('RECEPTIONIST'), false);
  assert.equal(canAdminManageClinics('DOCTOR'), false);

  assert.equal(canViewPatientList('DOCTOR'), true);
  assert.equal(canViewPatientList('RECEPTIONIST'), true);
  assert.equal(canViewPatientList('ANONYMOUS'), false);

  assert.equal(canConfirmBookings('RECEPTIONIST'), true);
  assert.equal(canConfirmBookings('DOCTOR'), false);

  // Receptionist CANNOT mark appointment as COMPLETED (Medical completion reserved for Doctor/Admin)
  assert.equal(canCompleteAppointment('RECEPTIONIST'), false);
  assert.equal(canCompleteAppointment('DOCTOR'), true);
  assert.equal(canCompleteAppointment('ADMIN'), true);

  // Receptionist CANNOT view audit logs (Admin only)
  assert.equal(canViewAuditLogs('RECEPTIONIST'), false);
  assert.equal(canViewAuditLogs('DOCTOR'), false);
  assert.equal(canViewAuditLogs('ADMIN'), true);
});

test('book_appointment_atomic business logic validations', () => {
  // 1. Date window validation
  const validateBookingDate = (dateStr: string) => {
    const today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    const target = new Date(dateStr);
    target.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

    const maxFuture = new Date(today);
    maxFuture.setDate(today.getDate() + 60);

    if (target < today) return { valid: false, error: 'past_date' };
    if (target > maxFuture) return { valid: false, error: 'too_far' };
    return { valid: true };
  };

  assert.equal(validateBookingDate('2020-01-01').valid, false);
  assert.equal(validateBookingDate('2020-01-01').error, 'past_date');

  // 2. Working days validation (e.g. clinic operates on days [0,1,2,3,4,6], Friday is 5)
  const isWorkingDay = (daysOfWeek: number[], dayOfWeek: number) => daysOfWeek.includes(dayOfWeek);
  const standardClinicDays = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 6];
  assert.equal(isWorkingDay(standardClinicDays, 0), true); // Sunday
  assert.equal(isWorkingDay(standardClinicDays, 5), false); // Friday closed

  // 3. Capacity enforcement logic
  const isWithinCapacity = (currentBooked: number, capacity: number) => currentBooked < capacity;
  assert.equal(isWithinCapacity(19, 20), true);
  assert.equal(isWithinCapacity(20, 20), false);
  assert.equal(isWithinCapacity(25, 20), false);
});

test('Invariant A1: Cairo timezone date filtering strictly excludes past dates', async () => {
  const { getTodayCairoDateString, isDateTodayOrFutureInCairo } = await import('./rules.ts');

  const todayCairo = getTodayCairoDateString();
  assert.match(todayCairo, /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/);

  // Today must be considered valid
  assert.equal(isDateTodayOrFutureInCairo(todayCairo), true);

  // Future dates (tomorrow, next week) must be valid
  const tomorrow = new Date();
  tomorrow.setDate(tomorrow.getDate() + 1);
  const tomorrowStr = tomorrow.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  assert.equal(isDateTodayOrFutureInCairo(tomorrowStr), true);

  // Past dates must strictly be rejected
  assert.equal(isDateTodayOrFutureInCairo('2020-01-01'), false);
  assert.equal(isDateTodayOrFutureInCairo('2025-12-31'), false);
});

  const { getTodayCairoDateString } = await import('./rules.ts');

    'dentistry',

      isClosed: false,

    }
  );

    clinicId: 'dentistry',

    phoneNumber: testPhone,

    patientAddress: 'أبو حمص',
    guardianNationalId: '29801011801234',

  });

    assert.notEqual(apt.status, 'CANCELLED');

    assert.equal((apt as any).guardianNationalId, undefined, 'guardianNationalId must NEVER be returned in tracking');

    assert.ok(apt.clinicName.length > 0);
    assert.ok(apt.serviceName && apt.serviceName.length > 0);
    assert.ok(Array.isArray(apt.requiredDocuments));
    assert.ok(typeof apt.serviceInstructions === 'string');
  }
});

  // Single word name

  assert.equal(maskPatientName('   '), '');
  assert.equal(maskPatientName(null), '');
  assert.equal(maskPatientName(undefined), '');
});

    appointmentDate: testDate,
  });

    patientName: 'نور أحمد علي حسن',
    phoneNumber: siblingPhone,
    patientAge: '2 سنة',

  const duplicateChild1 = await appointmentStore.createAppointment({
    clinicId: 'dentistry',
    patientName: 'ياسين أحمد علي حسن',
    phoneNumber: siblingPhone,
    appointmentDate: testDate,
  });
  assert.equal(duplicateChild1.success, false, 'Exact same child duplicate on same date must be rejected');
  assert.match(duplicateChild1.error || '', /يوجد حجز مسبق بالفعل/);
});
