export const PRECONFIGURED_MEDIA_ASSETS: MediaAsset[] = [
  { id: 'asset-1', name: 'شعار العيادات الخارجية', url: '/logo-outpatient.png', mime_type: 'image/png', is_custom: false },
  { id: 'asset-2', name: 'شعار المستشفى الرئيسي', url: '/logo-main.png', mime_type: 'image/png', is_custom: false },
  { id: 'asset-3', name: 'أيقونة المتصفح الافتراضية', url: '/favicon.ico', mime_type: 'image/x-icon', is_custom: false },
];

class CmsService {
  private settings: WebsiteSettings = { ...DEFAULT_WEBSITE_SETTINGS };
  private sections: HomepageSection[] = [...DEFAULT_HOMEPAGE_SECTIONS];
  private navigation: NavigationItem[] = [...DEFAULT_NAV_ITEMS];
  private clinicsPresentation: ClinicPresentation[] = [...DEFAULT_CLINICS_PRESENTATION];
  private customAssets: MediaAsset[] = [];

  public async getPublishedContent(): Promise<CmsFullPayload> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase: any = createAdminClient();
        const [settingsRes, sectionsRes, navRes, clinicsRes] = await Promise.all([
          supabase.from('website_settings').select('*').eq('id', 'default').single(),
          supabase.from('homepage_sections').select('*').order('display_order', { ascending: true }),
          supabase.from('navigation_items').select('*').order('display_order', { ascending: true }),
          supabase.from('clinics').select('id, name, description, location, working_hours, display_order, is_featured_on_homepage, english_title, accent_color, icon_name, is_hospital_confirmed, is_active').eq('is_active', true).order('display_order', { ascending: true }),
        ]);

        const settings = (settingsRes.data as unknown as WebsiteSettings) || this.settings;
        const sections = (sectionsRes.data as unknown as HomepageSection[]) || this.sections;
        const navigation = (navRes.data as unknown as NavigationItem[]) || this.navigation;
        const clinics = (clinicsRes.data as unknown as ClinicPresentation[]) || this.clinicsPresentation;

        return {
          settings,
          sections: sections.filter((s) => s.is_visible),
          navigation: navigation.filter((n) => n.is_visible),
          clinics: clinics.filter((c) => c.is_featured_on_homepage && c.is_active),
        };
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Supabase getPublishedContent fallback:', err);
      }
    }

    return {
      settings: this.settings,
      sections: this.sections.filter((s) => s.is_visible),
      navigation: this.navigation.filter((n) => n.is_visible),
      clinics: this.clinicsPresentation.filter((c) => c.is_featured_on_homepage && c.is_active),
    };
  }

  public async getCmsDashboardData(): Promise<CmsFullPayload & { assets: MediaAsset[] }> {
    let assets: MediaAsset[] = [...PRECONFIGURED_MEDIA_ASSETS, ...this.customAssets];

    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase: any = createAdminClient();
        const [settingsRes, sectionsRes, navRes, clinicsRes, mediaRes] = await Promise.all([
          supabase.from('website_settings').select('*').eq('id', 'default').single(),
          supabase.from('homepage_sections').select('*').order('display_order', { ascending: true }),
          supabase.from('navigation_items').select('*').order('display_order', { ascending: true }),
          supabase.from('clinics').select('id, name, description, location, working_hours, display_order, is_featured_on_homepage, english_title, accent_color, icon_name, is_hospital_confirmed, is_active').order('display_order', { ascending: true }),
          supabase.from('media_assets').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
        ]);

        if (settingsRes.data) this.settings = settingsRes.data as unknown as WebsiteSettings;
        if (sectionsRes.data && sectionsRes.data.length > 0) this.sections = sectionsRes.data as unknown as HomepageSection[];
        if (navRes.data && navRes.data.length > 0) this.navigation = navRes.data as unknown as NavigationItem[];
        if (clinicsRes.data && clinicsRes.data.length > 0) this.clinicsPresentation = clinicsRes.data as unknown as ClinicPresentation[];
        if (mediaRes.data && mediaRes.data.length > 0) {
          assets = mediaRes.data as unknown as MediaAsset[];
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Supabase getCmsDashboardData fallback:', err);
      }
    }

    return {
      settings: this.settings,
      sections: this.sections,
      navigation: this.navigation,
      clinics: this.clinicsPresentation,
      assets,
    };
  }

  public async updateSettings(
    updates: Partial<WebsiteSettings>,
    user: StaffUser
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; settings?: WebsiteSettings; error?: string }> {
    if (user.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return { success: false, error: 'غير مصرح بتعديل إعدادات وهوية الموقع. صلاحية مدير النظام مطلوبة.' };
    }

    const updated: WebsiteSettings = {
      ...this.settings,
      ...updates,
      id: 'default',
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_by: user.name,
      is_published: true,
      published_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase: any = createAdminClient();
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('website_settings')
          .upsert(updated as any, { onConflict: 'id' });

        if (error) {
          console.error('Supabase updateSettings failed with code:', error.code);
          return {
            success: false,
            error: 'فشل في حفظ إعدادات وهوية الموقع في قاعدة البيانات المركزية. يرجى مراجعة الصلاحيات والمحاولة لاحقاً.',
          };
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Supabase updateSettings exception:', err?.message || 'Network error');
        return {
          success: false,
          error: 'تعذر الاتصال بقاعدة البيانات المركزية لتحديث الإعدادات. يرجى التحقق من الشبكة.',
        };
      }
    } else if (!isMockStoreAllowed()) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'خدمة حفظ الإعدادات غير متصلة بقاعدة البيانات في بيئة الإنتاج.',
      };
    }

    this.settings = updated;
    await safeRevalidatePath('/');
    return { success: true, settings: this.settings };
  }

  public async updateSection(
    sectionId: string,
    updates: Partial<HomepageSection>,
    user: StaffUser
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; section?: HomepageSection; error?: string }> {
    if (user.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return { success: false, error: 'غير مصرح بتعديل أقسام الصفحة الرئيسية. صلاحية مدير النظام مطلوبة.' };
    }

    const idx = this.sections.findIndex((s) => s.id === sectionId);
    if (idx === -1) {
      return { success: false, error: 'القسم المطلوب غير موجود' };
    }

    const updated: HomepageSection = {
      ...this.sections[idx],
      ...updates,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_by: user.name,
    };

    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase: any = createAdminClient();
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('homepage_sections')
          .upsert(updated as any, { onConflict: 'id' });

        if (error) {
          console.error('Supabase updateSection failed with code:', error.code);
          return {
            success: false,
            error: `فشل في حفظ قسم (${updated.title || sectionId}) في قاعدة البيانات المركزية.`,
          };
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Supabase updateSection exception:', err?.message || 'Network error');
        return {
          success: false,
          error: 'تعذر الاتصال بقاعدة البيانات لتحديث القسم.',
        };
      }
    } else if (!isMockStoreAllowed()) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'خدمة تعديل الأقسام غير متصلة بقاعدة البيانات في بيئة الإنتاج.',
      };
    }

    this.sections[idx] = updated;
    await safeRevalidatePath('/');
    return { success: true, section: updated };
  }

  public async reorderSections(
    ordered: (string | { id: string; display_order: number })[],
    user: StaffUser
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; sections?: HomepageSection[]; error?: string }> {
    if (user.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return { success: false, error: 'صلاحية مدير النظام مطلوبة لإعادة ترتيب الأقسام.' };
    }

    ordered.forEach((item, idx) => {
      const id = typeof item === 'string' ? item : item.id;
      const sec = this.sections.find((s) => s.id === id);
      if (sec) {
        sec.display_order = typeof item === 'object' && item.display_order ? item.display_order : idx + 1;
      }
    });

    this.sections.sort((a, b) => a.display_order - b.display_order);

    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase: any = createAdminClient();
        for (const sec of this.sections) {
          const { error } = await supabase
            .from('homepage_sections')
            .update({ display_order: sec.display_order, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
            .eq('id', sec.id);

          if (error) {
            console.error('Supabase reorderSections failed on section:', sec.id, 'code:', error.code);
            return {
              success: false,
              error: 'فشل في تحديث ترتيب بعض الأقسام في قاعدة البيانات المركزية.',
            };
          }
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Supabase reorderSections exception:', err?.message || 'Network error');
        return {
          success: false,
          error: 'تعذر الاتصال بقاعدة البيانات لإعادة ترتيب الأقسام.',
        };
      }
    } else if (!isMockStoreAllowed()) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'خدمة ترتيب الأقسام غير متصلة بقاعدة البيانات في بيئة الإنتاج.',
      };
    }

    await safeRevalidatePath('/');
    return { success: true, sections: this.sections };
  }

  public async updateNavigation(
    items: NavigationItem[],
    user: StaffUser
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; navigation?: NavigationItem[]; error?: string }> {
    if (user.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return { success: false, error: 'صلاحية مدير النظام مطلوبة لتعديل قوائم التنقل.' };
    }

    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase: any = createAdminClient();
        for (const item of items) {
          const { error } = await supabase.from('navigation_items').upsert(item as any, { onConflict: 'id' });
          if (error) {
            console.error('Supabase updateNavigation failed on item:', item.id, 'code:', error.code);
            return {
              success: false,
              error: 'فشل في حفظ بعض عناصر القوائم في قاعدة البيانات المركزية.',
            };
          }
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Supabase updateNavigation exception:', err?.message || 'Network error');
        return {
          success: false,
          error: 'تعذر الاتصال بقاعدة البيانات لتحديث القوائم.',
        };
      }
    } else if (!isMockStoreAllowed()) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'خدمة تعديل القوائم غير متصلة بقاعدة البيانات في بيئة الإنتاج.',
      };
    }

    this.navigation = items;
    await safeRevalidatePath('/');
    return { success: true, navigation: this.navigation };
  }

  public async updateClinicPresentation(
    clinicId: string,
    updates: Partial<ClinicPresentation>,
    user: StaffUser
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; clinic?: ClinicPresentation; error?: string }> {
    if (user.role !== 'ADMIN') {
      return { success: false, error: 'صلاحية مدير النظام مطلوبة لتعديل بيانات عرض العيادة.' };
    }

    const clinic = this.clinicsPresentation.find((c) => c.id === clinicId);
    if (!clinic) {
      return { success: false, error: 'العيادة المطلوبة غير موجودة' };
    }

    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase: any = createAdminClient();
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('clinics')
          .update({
            display_order: updates.display_order !== undefined ? updates.display_order : clinic.display_order,
            is_featured_on_homepage: updates.is_featured_on_homepage !== undefined ? updates.is_featured_on_homepage : clinic.is_featured_on_homepage,
            english_title: updates.english_title !== undefined ? updates.english_title : clinic.english_title,
            accent_color: updates.accent_color !== undefined ? updates.accent_color : clinic.accent_color,
            icon_name: updates.icon_name !== undefined ? updates.icon_name : clinic.icon_name,
            description: updates.description !== undefined ? updates.description : clinic.description,
            instructions: updates.instructions !== undefined ? updates.instructions : clinic.instructions,
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
          .eq('id', clinicId);

        if (error) {
          console.error('Supabase updateClinicPresentation failed with code:', error.code);
          return {
            success: false,
            error: `فشل في تحديث مظهر عيادة (${clinic.name}) في قاعدة البيانات المركزية.`,
          };
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Supabase updateClinicPresentation exception:', err?.message || 'Network error');
        return {
          success: false,
          error: 'تعذر الاتصال بقاعدة البيانات لتحديث مظهر العيادة.',
        };
      }
    } else if (!isMockStoreAllowed()) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'خدمة تعديل مظهر العيادات غير متصلة بقاعدة البيانات في بيئة الإنتاج.',
      };
    }

    // Safety constraint: apply updates to cache only after DB confirms (or in permitted mock mode)
    if (updates.display_order !== undefined) clinic.display_order = updates.display_order;
    if (updates.is_featured_on_homepage !== undefined) clinic.is_featured_on_homepage = updates.is_featured_on_homepage;
    if (updates.english_title !== undefined) clinic.english_title = updates.english_title;
    if (updates.accent_color !== undefined) clinic.accent_color = updates.accent_color;
    if (updates.icon_name !== undefined) clinic.icon_name = updates.icon_name;
    if (updates.description !== undefined) clinic.description = updates.description;
    if (updates.instructions !== undefined) clinic.instructions = updates.instructions;

    await safeRevalidatePath('/');
    return { success: true, clinic };
  }

  public registerCustomAsset(asset: MediaAsset) {
    this.customAssets.unshift(asset);
  }
}

const globalForCms = globalThis as unknown as {
  cmsServiceInstance?: CmsService;
};

export const cmsService = globalForCms.cmsServiceInstance ?? new CmsService();
if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
  globalForCms.cmsServiceInstance = cmsService;
}



===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\diagnostics\00_read_only_audit.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بالبحيرة
-- Diagnostic Script: Read-Only Database Security & Schema Audit
-- File: supabase/diagnostics/00_read_only_audit.sql
-- ========================================================================
--
-- IMPORTANT:
-- This script contains ONLY read-only SELECT queries.
-- It does NOT modify, drop, insert, or alter any table or security policy.
-- Run this in the Supabase SQL Editor and review the results to evaluate the
-- actual state of the remote database before applying proposed RLS migrations.
-- ========================================================================

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Table RLS Status (هل RLS مفعل على كل جدول في schema public؟)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
SELECT 
  schemaname, 
  tablename, 
  rowsecurity AS is_rls_enabled
FROM pg_tables
WHERE schemaname = 'public'
ORDER BY tablename;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Active Row Level Security (RLS) Policies (جميع السياسات المفعلة وشروطها)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
SELECT 
  schemaname,
  tablename,
  policyname,
  permissive,
  roles,
  cmd,
  qual AS using_expression,
  with_check AS with_check_expression
FROM pg_policies
WHERE schemaname = 'public'
ORDER BY tablename, policyname;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Privilege Grants to anon and authenticated Roles on Public Tables
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
SELECT 
  table_schema,
  table_name,
  grantee,
  privilege_type
FROM information_schema.role_table_grants
WHERE table_schema = 'public'
  AND grantee IN ('anon', 'authenticated', 'public')
ORDER BY table_name, grantee, privilege_type;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. Function Definitions Used in Security & Policies
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
SELECT 
  p.proname AS function_name,
  pg_get_userbyid(p.proowner) AS owner_role,
  p.prosecdef AS is_security_definer,
  p.provolatile AS volatility_category,
  pg_get_functiondef(p.oid) AS definition
FROM pg_proc p
JOIN pg_namespace n ON p.pronamespace = n.oid
WHERE n.nspname = 'public'
  AND p.proname IN (
    'get_current_staff_role',
    'is_active_staff',
    'get_doctor_clinic_id',
    'handle_updated_at',
    'provision_staff_member',
    'check_appointment_update_permissions'
  )
ORDER BY p.proname;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 5. Existing Columns in the 6 Core Tables
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
SELECT 
  table_name,
  column_name,
  data_type,
  is_nullable,
  column_default
FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'public'
  AND table_name IN (
    'clinics',
    'clinic_schedules',
    'appointments',
    'appointment_status_history',
    'staff_profiles',
    'audit_logs'
  )
ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position;



===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\migrations\20261024000000_init_bsch_clinics.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بالبحيرة
-- Supabase PostgreSQL Relational Schema, Row Level Security (RLS) & RPCs
-- Migration: 20261024000000_init_bsch_clinics.sql
-- ========================================================================

-- Enable core extensions safely
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Custom Types & Enums
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE staff_role AS ENUM ('ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST', 'DOCTOR');
EXCEPTION
  WHEN duplicate_object THEN null;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE appointment_status AS ENUM (
    'PENDING_REVIEW',  -- قيد المراجعة (طلب إلكتروني بانتظار الاعتماد)
    'CONFIRMED',       -- مؤكد (تم اعتماد الموعد وتخصيص رقم الدور)
    'CHECKED_IN',      -- حضر المريض (في قائمة الاستدعاء وشاشة الانتظار)
    'COMPLETED',       -- مكتمل (تم الكشف الطبي وإغلاق التذكرة)
    'CANCELLED'        -- ملغي
  );
EXCEPTION
  WHEN duplicate_object THEN null;
END $$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Clinics Table (العيادات الخارجية)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.clinics (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  location TEXT NOT NULL,
  working_hours TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '08:30-14:00',
  days_of_week INTEGER[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{0,1,2,3,4,6}', -- الأحد للخميس والسبت (0=Sun .. 6=Sat)
  max_daily_slots INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 20,
  slot_duration_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 30,
  instructions TEXT,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  is_hospital_confirmed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false, -- للتمييز الصريح بين البيانات التجريبية والمعتمدة رسمياً
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  CONSTRAINT chk_max_daily_slots CHECK (max_daily_slots > 0),
  CONSTRAINT chk_slot_duration CHECK (slot_duration_minutes > 0)
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Staff Profiles (ملفات موظفي وأطباء المستشفى)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.staff_profiles (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  role staff_role NOT NULL DEFAULT 'RECEPTIONIST',
  clinic_id TEXT REFERENCES public.clinics(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. Clinic Schedules & Overrides (جداول العيادات والعطلات والسعات الاستثنائية)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.clinic_schedules (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  clinic_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES public.clinics(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  schedule_date DATE NOT NULL,
  max_capacity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 20,
  is_closed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  closure_reason TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  CONSTRAINT unique_clinic_date UNIQUE (clinic_id, schedule_date),
  CONSTRAINT chk_schedule_capacity CHECK (max_capacity >= 0)
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 5. Appointments Table (الحجوزات والمواعيد)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.appointments (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  reference_number TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL, -- e.g. BSCH-7492
  clinic_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES public.clinics(id),
  clinic_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  patient_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  phone_number VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL, -- Egyptian 11 digits
  patient_age TEXT,
  patient_address TEXT,
  appointment_date DATE NOT NULL,
  appointment_time TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '09:00',
  status appointment_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING_REVIEW',
  queue_number INTEGER,
  last_called_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  called_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  notes TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  CONSTRAINT chk_phone_number CHECK (phone_number ~ '^01[0125][0-9]{8}$'),
  CONSTRAINT chk_patient_name CHECK (length(trim(patient_name)) >= 5),
  CONSTRAINT chk_reference_number CHECK (reference_number ~ '^BSCH-[0-9]{4,6}$'),
  CONSTRAINT chk_queue_number CHECK (queue_number IS NULL OR queue_number > 0)
);

-- Partial Unique Index: يمنع تكرار أي حجز نشط لنفس رقم الهاتف في نفس العيادة ونفس اليوم
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_clinic_day
  ON public.appointments (clinic_id, phone_number, appointment_date)
  WHERE (status != 'CANCELLED');

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 6. Appointment Status History (سجل تغير الحالات وتدفق الخدمة)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.appointment_status_history (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  appointment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.appointments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  status appointment_status NOT NULL,
  note TEXT,
  updated_by TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 7. Audit Logs (سجل العمليات الإدارية الحساسة والرقابة)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.audit_logs (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  action TEXT NOT NULL,
  entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  details JSONB,
  ip_address TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Performance Indexes
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_appointments_phone ON public.appointments (phone_number);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_appointments_ref ON public.appointments (reference_number);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_appointments_date_clinic ON public.appointments (clinic_id, appointment_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_appointments_status ON public.appointments (status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_status_history_apt ON public.appointment_status_history (appointment_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clinic_schedules_lookup ON public.clinic_schedules (clinic_id, schedule_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_logs_user ON public.audit_logs (user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_logs_entity ON public.audit_logs (entity_type, entity_id);

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 8. Updated At Triggers
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = timezone('utc'::text, now());
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_clinics_updated_at ON public.clinics;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_clinics_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.clinics
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_updated_at();

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_staff_profiles_updated_at ON public.staff_profiles;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_staff_profiles_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.staff_profiles
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_updated_at();

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_clinic_schedules_updated_at ON public.clinic_schedules;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_clinic_schedules_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.clinic_schedules
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_updated_at();

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_appointments_updated_at ON public.appointments;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_appointments_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.appointments
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_updated_at();

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 9. Security Definer Helper Functions
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Helper: Check if current user is an active staff member
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_active_staff()
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.staff_profiles
    WHERE id = auth.uid() AND is_active = true
  );
$$;

-- Helper: Get current staff user role
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_current_staff_role()
RETURNS staff_role
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT role FROM public.staff_profiles
  WHERE id = auth.uid() AND is_active = true
  LIMIT 1;
$$;

-- Helper: Get current doctor assigned clinic id (strictly requires active doctor with non-null clinic_id)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
RETURNS TEXT
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT clinic_id FROM public.staff_profiles
  WHERE id = auth.uid()
    AND is_active = true
    AND role = 'DOCTOR'
    AND clinic_id IS NOT NULL
  LIMIT 1;
$$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 10. Receptionist & Doctor Update Privilege Guard Trigger
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.check_appointment_update_permissions()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_role staff_role;
  v_doc_clinic TEXT;
BEGIN
  v_role := public.get_current_staff_role();

  -- Allow Admin and service_role unconstrained administrative maintenance
  IF v_role = 'ADMIN' OR auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  -- 1. Reference number is strictly immutable
  IF OLD.reference_number != NEW.reference_number THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'الرقم المرجعي للحجز غير قابل للتعديل نهائياً.';
  END IF;

  -- 2. Terminal state protection
  IF OLD.status = 'COMPLETED' AND NEW.status != 'COMPLETED' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'لا يمكن تعديل أو إعادة فتح حجز مكتمل تم الكشف عليه مسبقاً.';
  END IF;

  IF OLD.status = 'CANCELLED' AND NEW.status != 'CANCELLED' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'لا يمكن تعديل أو إعادة فتح حجز ملغي مسبقاً.';
  END IF;

  -- 3. Receptionist role restrictions
  IF v_role = 'RECEPTIONIST' THEN
    -- Receptionist cannot change core identity or appointment slot
    IF OLD.patient_name != NEW.patient_name
       OR OLD.phone_number != NEW.phone_number
       OR OLD.clinic_id != NEW.clinic_id
       OR OLD.appointment_date != NEW.appointment_date THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح لموظف الاستقبال بتعديل بيانات المريض أو العيادة أو تاريخ الموعد مباشرة. يلزم إجراء تحويل رسمي أو مراجعة مدير النظام.';
    END IF;

    -- Receptionist cannot complete appointments (medical evaluation is doctor only)
    IF NEW.status = 'COMPLETED' THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'إتمام الكشف الطبي وصرف التذكرة صلاحية طبية مخصصة للأطباء فقط.';
    END IF;
  END IF;

  -- 4. Doctor role restrictions
  IF v_role = 'DOCTOR' THEN
    v_doc_clinic := public.get_doctor_clinic_id();
    IF v_doc_clinic IS NULL OR OLD.clinic_id != v_doc_clinic THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح للطبيب بتعديل مواعيد خاصة بعيادة أخرى أو بدون تعيين عيادة معتمدة.';
    END IF;

    -- Doctor cannot tamper with patient identity or appointment date
    IF OLD.patient_name != NEW.patient_name
       OR OLD.phone_number != NEW.phone_number
       OR OLD.appointment_date != NEW.appointment_date THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح للطبيب بتعديل البيانات الشخصية أو تاريخ الحجز.';
    END IF;
  END IF;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_check_appointment_update_permissions ON public.appointments;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_appointment_update_permissions
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.appointments
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.check_appointment_update_permissions();

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 11. Row Level Security (RLS) Configuration
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Enable RLS on all tables
ALTER TABLE public.clinics ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.staff_profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.clinic_schedules ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.appointments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.appointment_status_history ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.audit_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Revoke default public schema grants to ensure zero leakage to anonymous role
REVOKE ALL ON public.appointments FROM anon;
REVOKE ALL ON public.appointment_status_history FROM anon;
REVOKE ALL ON public.audit_logs FROM anon;
REVOKE ALL ON public.staff_profiles FROM anon;

-- Explicit table grants
GRANT SELECT ON public.clinics TO anon, authenticated;
GRANT SELECT ON public.clinic_schedules TO anon, authenticated;
GRANT SELECT, UPDATE ON public.appointments TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON public.appointment_status_history TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON public.audit_logs TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.staff_profiles TO authenticated;

-- RLS: clinics
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Public can view active clinics" ON public.clinics;
CREATE POLICY "Public can view active clinics"
  ON public.clinics FOR SELECT
  USING (is_active = true OR public.is_active_staff());

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins can manage clinics" ON public.clinics;
CREATE POLICY "Admins can manage clinics"
  ON public.clinics FOR ALL
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN')
  WITH CHECK (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN');

-- RLS: clinic_schedules
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Public can view schedules" ON public.clinic_schedules;
CREATE POLICY "Public can view schedules"
  ON public.clinic_schedules FOR SELECT
  USING (true);

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins can manage schedules" ON public.clinic_schedules;
CREATE POLICY "Admins can manage schedules"
  ON public.clinic_schedules FOR ALL
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN')
  WITH CHECK (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN');

-- RLS: staff_profiles
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can view own profile or admin view all" ON public.staff_profiles;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can view own profile or admin view all"
  ON public.staff_profiles FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (id = auth.uid() OR public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN');

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins manage staff profiles" ON public.staff_profiles;
CREATE POLICY "Admins manage staff profiles"
  ON public.staff_profiles FOR ALL
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN')
  WITH CHECK (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN');

-- RLS: appointments
-- Anonymous visitors CANNOT run SELECT directly against appointments table!
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can view appointments" ON public.appointments;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can view appointments"
  ON public.appointments FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() IN ('ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST')
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
      )
    )
  );

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can update appointments" ON public.appointments;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can update appointments"
  ON public.appointments FOR UPDATE
  TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN'
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
      )
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'RECEPTIONIST'
        AND status != 'COMPLETED'
      )
    )
  )
  WITH CHECK (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN'
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
      )
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'RECEPTIONIST'
        AND status != 'COMPLETED'
      )
    )
  );

-- RLS: appointment_status_history
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can view status history" ON public.appointment_status_history;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can view status history"
  ON public.appointment_status_history FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() IN ('ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST')
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM public.appointments a
          WHERE a.id = appointment_id
            AND a.clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
        )
      )
    )
  );

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can insert status history" ON public.appointment_status_history;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can insert status history"
  ON public.appointment_status_history FOR INSERT
  TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() IN ('ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST')
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM public.appointments a
          WHERE a.id = appointment_id
            AND a.clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
        )
      )
    )
  );

-- Note: NO UPDATE OR DELETE policies on appointment_status_history (Immutable audit history)

-- RLS: audit_logs
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins can view audit logs" ON public.audit_logs;
CREATE POLICY "Admins can view audit logs"
  ON public.audit_logs FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN');

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Active staff can insert audit logs" ON public.audit_logs;
CREATE POLICY "Active staff can insert audit logs"
  ON public.audit_logs FOR INSERT
  TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (public.is_active_staff());

-- Note: NO UPDATE OR DELETE policies on audit_logs (Immutable audit log)

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 12. Atomic Booking Function (book_appointment_atomic) with Data Minimization
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(
  p_clinic_id TEXT,
  p_patient_name TEXT,
  p_phone_number TEXT,
  p_patient_age TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_patient_address TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_appointment_date DATE DEFAULT NULL,
  p_appointment_time TEXT DEFAULT '09:00',
  p_reference_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_clinic RECORD;
  v_schedule RECORD;
  v_effective_capacity INTEGER;
  v_current_booked INTEGER;
  v_queue_number INTEGER;
  v_ref TEXT;
  v_new_appointment RECORD;
  v_is_staff BOOLEAN;
  v_initial_status appointment_status;
  v_clean_phone TEXT;
  v_clean_name TEXT;
  v_dow INTEGER;
BEGIN
  -- 1. Validate and clean input fields
  v_clean_phone := regexp_replace(trim(COALESCE(p_phone_number, '')), '\s+', '', 'g');
  v_clean_name := trim(COALESCE(p_patient_name, ''));

  IF v_clean_phone !~ '^01[0125][0-9]{8}$' THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'رقم الهاتف غير صالح. يجب إدخال رقم محمول مصري صحيح مكون من 11 رقماً (010, 011, 012, 015).');
  END IF;

  IF length(v_clean_name) < 5 OR array_length(regexp_split_to_array(v_clean_name, '\s+'), 1) < 2 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'يرجى كتابة اسم المريض ثنائياً على الأقل (ويفضل رباعي).');
  END IF;

  IF p_appointment_date IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'تاريخ الموعد مطلوب.');
  END IF;

  IF p_appointment_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'لا يمكن حجز موعد في تاريخ سابق.');
  END IF;

  IF p_appointment_date > (CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '60 days') THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'الحجز الإلكتروني متاح بحد أقصى 60 يوماً مقدماً.');
  END IF;

  -- 2. Concurrency Safety: Lock the clinic and date transactionally
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('booking_' || p_clinic_id || '_' || p_appointment_date::text));

  -- 3. Validate Clinic
  SELECT * INTO v_clinic FROM public.clinics WHERE id = p_clinic_id;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المختارة غير متوفرة بالنظام.');
  END IF;

  IF NOT v_clinic.is_active THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المختارة متوقفة مؤقتاً عن استقبال الحجوزات.');
  END IF;

  -- 4. Validate clinic working days
  v_dow := EXTRACT(DOW FROM p_appointment_date)::INTEGER;
  IF NOT (v_dow = ANY(v_clinic.days_of_week)) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المختارة لا تعمل في هذا اليوم من الأسبوع.');
  END IF;

  -- 5. Check schedule overrides (holidays, special closures, custom capacity)
  SELECT * INTO v_schedule FROM public.clinic_schedules
  WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id AND schedule_date = p_appointment_date;

  IF FOUND AND v_schedule.is_closed THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
      'success', false,
      'error', 'العيادة مغلقة في هذا التاريخ' || COALESCE(': ' || v_schedule.closure_reason, ' نظراً لعطلة رسمية أو ظرف طارئ.')
    );
  END IF;

  v_effective_capacity := COALESCE(v_schedule.max_capacity, v_clinic.max_daily_slots);

  -- 6. Check duplicate reservation for same phone in same clinic on the same day
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.appointments
    WHERE phone_number = v_clean_phone
      AND clinic_id = p_clinic_id
      AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date
      AND status != 'CANCELLED'
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'يوجد حجز مسبق بالفعل لهذا الرقم في نفس العيادة والتاريخ.');
  END IF;

  -- 7. Check capacity
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_current_booked
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id
    AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date
    AND status != 'CANCELLED';

  IF v_current_booked >= v_effective_capacity THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
      'success', false,
      'error', format('عفواً، اكتملت السعة اليومية المتاحة للعيادة في هذا التاريخ (%s مريض). يرجى اختيار موعد آخر.', v_effective_capacity)
    );
  END IF;

  -- 8. Calculate monotonic queue number
  SELECT COALESCE(MAX(queue_number), 0) + 1 INTO v_queue_number
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id
    AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date;

  -- 9. Determine initial status based on caller (walk-in staff vs online portal)
  v_is_staff := (auth.uid() IS NOT NULL) AND public.is_active_staff();
  IF v_is_staff THEN
    v_initial_status := 'CONFIRMED';
  ELSE
    v_initial_status := 'PENDING_REVIEW';
  END IF;

  -- 10. Guarantee unique reference number
  v_ref := trim(COALESCE(p_reference_number, ''));
  IF v_ref = '' OR v_ref !~ '^BSCH-[0-9]{4,6}$' OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.appointments WHERE reference_number = v_ref) THEN
    LOOP
      v_ref := 'BSCH-' || (floor(random() * 9000) + 1000)::text;
      EXIT WHEN NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.appointments WHERE reference_number = v_ref);
    END LOOP;
  END IF;

  -- 11. Insert appointment atomically
  INSERT INTO public.appointments (
    reference_number,
    clinic_id,
    clinic_name,
    patient_name,
    phone_number,
    patient_age,
    patient_address,
    appointment_date,
    appointment_time,
    status,
    queue_number,
    notes
  ) VALUES (
    v_ref,
    p_clinic_id,
    v_clinic.name,
    v_clean_name,
    v_clean_phone,
    trim(p_patient_age),
    trim(p_patient_address),
    p_appointment_date,
    COALESCE(trim(p_appointment_time), '09:00'),
    v_initial_status,
    v_queue_number,
    CASE WHEN v_is_staff THEN 'تسجيل حضوري مباشر من مكتب الاستقبال' ELSE NULL END
  ) RETURNING * INTO v_new_appointment;

  -- 12. Insert initial status history
  INSERT INTO public.appointment_status_history (
    appointment_id,
    status,
    note,
    updated_by
  ) VALUES (
    v_new_appointment.id,
    v_initial_status,
    CASE
      WHEN v_is_staff THEN 'تم تسجيل وتأكيد الحجز فورياً عبر مكتب الاستقبال'
      ELSE 'تم تسجيل طلب الحجز بنجاح عبر البوابة الإلكترونية وبانتظار المراجعة'
    END,
    CASE WHEN v_is_staff THEN 'موظف الاستقبال' ELSE 'بوابة الحجز الإلكترونية' END
  );

  -- 13. DATA MINIMIZATION: Return ONLY minimal fields to the client
  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'appointment', jsonb_build_object(
      'reference_number', v_new_appointment.reference_number,
      'clinic_id', v_new_appointment.clinic_id,
      'clinic_name', v_new_appointment.clinic_name,
      'patient_name', v_new_appointment.patient_name,
      'phone_number', v_new_appointment.phone_number,
      'appointment_date', v_new_appointment.appointment_date,
      'appointment_time', v_new_appointment.appointment_time,
      'status', v_new_appointment.status,
      'queue_number', v_new_appointment.queue_number
    )
  );
END;
$$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 13. Hardened Two-Factor Tracking RPC Function (track_appointment_secure)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Strictly requires BOTH Reference Number AND matching Phone Number.
-- Rejects queries with only phone or only reference.
-- Returns ONLY minimal tracking fields (TABLE of 8 fields), preventing any
-- exposure of patient address, age, medical notes, or unmasked database internals.
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(
  p_reference_number TEXT,
  p_phone_number TEXT
)
RETURNS TABLE (
  reference_number TEXT,
  clinic_id TEXT,
  clinic_name TEXT,
  patient_name TEXT,
  appointment_date DATE,
  appointment_time TEXT,
  status appointment_status,
  queue_number INTEGER
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_clean_ref TEXT;
  v_clean_phone TEXT;
BEGIN
  -- 1. Validate both inputs exist
  v_clean_ref := trim(upper(COALESCE(p_reference_number, '')));
  v_clean_phone := regexp_replace(trim(COALESCE(p_phone_number, '')), '\D', '', 'g');

  IF v_clean_ref = '' OR v_clean_phone = '' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  -- Prepend BSCH- if 4-6 digits provided
  IF v_clean_ref ~ '^[0-9]{4,6}$' THEN
    v_clean_ref := 'BSCH-' || v_clean_ref;
  END IF;

  -- Validate reference number format
  IF v_clean_ref !~ '^BSCH-[0-9]{4,6}$' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  -- Normalize phone (handle missing 0)
  IF length(v_clean_phone) = 10 AND v_clean_phone ~ '^(10|11|12|15)' THEN
    v_clean_phone := '0' || v_clean_phone;
  END IF;

  -- Validate Egyptian phone format
  IF v_clean_phone !~ '^01[0125][0-9]{8}$' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  -- 2. Query matching active appointment strictly requiring BOTH ref AND phone
  RETURN QUERY
  SELECT
    a.reference_number,
    a.clinic_id,
    a.clinic_name,
    a.patient_name,
    a.appointment_date,
    a.appointment_time,
    a.status,
    a.queue_number
  FROM public.appointments a
  WHERE a.reference_number = v_clean_ref
    AND a.phone_number = v_clean_phone
  ORDER BY a.appointment_date DESC, a.created_at DESC
  LIMIT 1;

  RETURN;
END;
$$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 14. Function Execution Privileges (RPC Permission Hardening)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Revoke all function executions from public
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_active_staff() FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_current_staff_role() FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_doctor_clinic_id() FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;

-- Grant to appropriate roles
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_active_staff() TO authenticated, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_current_staff_role() TO authenticated, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_doctor_clinic_id() TO authenticated, service_role;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT, TEXT) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\migrations\20261025000000_security_and_privilege_hardening.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بالبحيرة
-- Security & Privilege Hardening Migration (Non-Destructive)
-- Migration: 20261025000000_security_and_privilege_hardening.sql
-- ========================================================================
--
-- This migration applies the following security and schema hardenings idempotently:
-- 1. Adds missing columns safely without dropping or re-creating tables.
-- 2. Restricts Doctor RLS policies so unassigned doctors (clinic_id IS NULL) CANNOT access any clinic.
-- 3. Enforces Receptionist privilege boundaries (cannot modify core patient data, clinic, date, or mark COMPLETED).
-- 4. Secures `track_appointment_secure` to require BOTH Reference Number AND Phone Number, returning minimized fields only.
-- 5. Minimizes data returned by `book_appointment_atomic` to visitors.
-- 6. Hardens RPC execute permissions and search paths.
-- ========================================================================

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Non-destructive column additions & constraints
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Clinics: hospital confirmation flag
ALTER TABLE public.clinics
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_hospital_confirmed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false;

-- Appointments: queue call tracking columns
ALTER TABLE public.appointments
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS last_called_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS called_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

-- Partial Unique Index: prevent duplicate active appointments for same phone in same clinic on same date
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_clinic_day
  ON public.appointments (clinic_id, phone_number, appointment_date)
  WHERE (status != 'CANCELLED');

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Security Definer Helper Functions Hardening
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Helper: Get current doctor assigned clinic id (strictly requires active doctor with non-null clinic_id)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
RETURNS TEXT
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT clinic_id FROM public.staff_profiles
  WHERE id = auth.uid()
    AND is_active = true
    AND role = 'DOCTOR'
    AND clinic_id IS NOT NULL
  LIMIT 1;
$$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Receptionist & Doctor Update Privilege Guard Trigger
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Enforces that:
-- - Receptionists CANNOT alter patient name, phone, address, age, clinic, or date directly.
-- - Receptionists CANNOT mark appointment as COMPLETED (Doctor / Admin only).
-- - Doctors CANNOT alter appointments of other clinics or modify patient personal data.
-- - Completed or Cancelled appointments cannot be reopened except by Admin.
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.check_appointment_update_permissions()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_role staff_role;
  v_doc_clinic TEXT;
BEGIN
  v_role := public.get_current_staff_role();

  -- Allow Admin and service_role unconstrained administrative maintenance
  IF v_role = 'ADMIN' OR auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  -- 1. Reference number is strictly immutable
  IF OLD.reference_number != NEW.reference_number THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'الرقم المرجعي للحجز غير قابل للتعديل نهائياً.';
  END IF;

  -- 2. Terminal state protection
  IF OLD.status = 'COMPLETED' AND NEW.status != 'COMPLETED' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'لا يمكن تعديل أو إعادة فتح حجز مكتمل تم الكشف عليه مسبقاً.';
  END IF;

  IF OLD.status = 'CANCELLED' AND NEW.status != 'CANCELLED' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'لا يمكن تعديل أو إعادة فتح حجز ملغي مسبقاً.';
  END IF;

  -- 3. Receptionist role restrictions
  IF v_role = 'RECEPTIONIST' THEN
    -- Receptionist cannot change core identity or appointment slot
    IF OLD.patient_name != NEW.patient_name
       OR OLD.phone_number != NEW.phone_number
       OR OLD.clinic_id != NEW.clinic_id
       OR OLD.appointment_date != NEW.appointment_date THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح لموظف الاستقبال بتعديل بيانات المريض أو العيادة أو تاريخ الموعد مباشرة. يلزم إجراء تحويل رسمي أو مراجعة مدير النظام.';
    END IF;

    -- Receptionist cannot complete appointments (medical evaluation is doctor only)
    IF NEW.status = 'COMPLETED' THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'إتمام الكشف الطبي وصرف التذكرة صلاحية طبية مخصصة للأطباء فقط.';
    END IF;
  END IF;

  -- 4. Doctor role restrictions
  IF v_role = 'DOCTOR' THEN
    v_doc_clinic := public.get_doctor_clinic_id();
    IF v_doc_clinic IS NULL OR OLD.clinic_id != v_doc_clinic THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح للطبيب بتعديل مواعيد خاصة بعيادة أخرى أو بدون تعيين عيادة معتمدة.';
    END IF;

    -- Doctor cannot tamper with patient identity or appointment date
    IF OLD.patient_name != NEW.patient_name
       OR OLD.phone_number != NEW.phone_number
       OR OLD.appointment_date != NEW.appointment_date THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح للطبيب بتعديل البيانات الشخصية أو تاريخ الحجز.';
    END IF;
  END IF;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_check_appointment_update_permissions ON public.appointments;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_appointment_update_permissions
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.appointments
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.check_appointment_update_permissions();

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. Hardened Row Level Security (RLS) Policies on Appointments
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Ensure anonymous role has ZERO direct access to appointments
REVOKE ALL ON public.appointments FROM anon;

-- Staff SELECT Policy:
-- - Admin & Receptionist can view appointments
-- - Doctor can ONLY view appointments for their active assigned clinic (NEVER null clinic_id)
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can view appointments" ON public.appointments;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can view appointments"
  ON public.appointments FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() IN ('ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST')
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
      )
    )
  );

-- Staff UPDATE Policy:
-- - Admin can update any appointment
-- - Doctor can ONLY update their assigned clinic's appointments
-- - Receptionist can update appointments except those marked COMPLETED
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can update appointments" ON public.appointments;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can update appointments"
  ON public.appointments FOR UPDATE
  TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN'
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
      )
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'RECEPTIONIST'
        AND status != 'COMPLETED'
      )
    )
  )
  WITH CHECK (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN'
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
      )
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'RECEPTIONIST'
        AND status != 'COMPLETED'
      )
    )
  );

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 5. Hardened Atomic Booking with Data Minimization
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Returns ONLY necessary booking information to the caller (reference_number,
-- clinic_name, patient_name, appointment_date, appointment_time, status, queue_number).
-- Omits internal UUIDs, patient address, age, and internal notes.
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(
  p_clinic_id TEXT,
  p_patient_name TEXT,
  p_phone_number TEXT,
  p_patient_age TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_patient_address TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_appointment_date DATE DEFAULT NULL,
  p_appointment_time TEXT DEFAULT '09:00',
  p_reference_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_clinic RECORD;
  v_schedule RECORD;
  v_effective_capacity INTEGER;
  v_current_booked INTEGER;
  v_queue_number INTEGER;
  v_ref TEXT;
  v_new_appointment RECORD;
  v_is_staff BOOLEAN;
  v_initial_status appointment_status;
  v_clean_phone TEXT;
  v_clean_name TEXT;
  v_dow INTEGER;
BEGIN
  -- 1. Clean & validate inputs
  v_clean_phone := regexp_replace(trim(COALESCE(p_phone_number, '')), '\s+', '', 'g');
  v_clean_name := trim(COALESCE(p_patient_name, ''));

  IF v_clean_phone !~ '^01[0125][0-9]{8}$' THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'رقم الهاتف غير صالح. يجب إدخال رقم محمول مصري صحيح مكون من 11 رقماً (010, 011, 012, 015).');
  END IF;

  IF length(v_clean_name) < 5 OR array_length(regexp_split_to_array(v_clean_name, '\s+'), 1) < 2 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'يرجى كتابة اسم المريض ثنائياً على الأقل (ويفضل رباعي).');
  END IF;

  IF p_appointment_date IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'تاريخ الموعد مطلوب.');
  END IF;

  IF p_appointment_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'لا يمكن حجز موعد في تاريخ سابق.');
  END IF;

  IF p_appointment_date > (CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '60 days') THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'الحجز الإلكتروني متاح بحد أقصى 60 يوماً مقدماً.');
  END IF;

  -- 2. Concurrency Safety: Transactional Advisory Lock per (clinic_id, appointment_date)
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('booking_' || p_clinic_id || '_' || p_appointment_date::text));

  -- 3. Validate Clinic
  SELECT * INTO v_clinic FROM public.clinics WHERE id = p_clinic_id;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المختارة غير متوفرة بالنظام.');
  END IF;

  IF NOT v_clinic.is_active THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المختارة متوقفة مؤقتاً عن استقبال الحجوزات.');
  END IF;

  -- 4. Validate working days
  v_dow := EXTRACT(DOW FROM p_appointment_date)::INTEGER;
  IF NOT (v_dow = ANY(v_clinic.days_of_week)) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المختارة لا تعمل في هذا اليوم من الأسبوع.');
  END IF;

  -- 5. Check schedule overrides & holidays
  SELECT * INTO v_schedule FROM public.clinic_schedules
  WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id AND schedule_date = p_appointment_date;

  IF FOUND AND v_schedule.is_closed THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
      'success', false,
      'error', 'العيادة مغلقة في هذا التاريخ' || COALESCE(': ' || v_schedule.closure_reason, ' نظراً لعطلة رسمية أو ظرف طارئ.')
    );
  END IF;

  v_effective_capacity := COALESCE(v_schedule.max_capacity, v_clinic.max_daily_slots);

  -- 6. Check duplicate active reservation
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.appointments
    WHERE phone_number = v_clean_phone
      AND clinic_id = p_clinic_id
      AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date
      AND status != 'CANCELLED'
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'يوجد حجز مسبق بالفعل لهذا الرقم في نفس العيادة والتاريخ.');
  END IF;

  -- 7. Check capacity
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_current_booked
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id
    AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date
    AND status != 'CANCELLED';

  IF v_current_booked >= v_effective_capacity THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
      'success', false,
      'error', format('عفواً، اكتملت السعة اليومية المتاحة للعيادة في هذا التاريخ (%s مريض). يرجى اختيار موعد آخر.', v_effective_capacity)
    );
  END IF;

  -- 8. Calculate monotonic queue number
  SELECT COALESCE(MAX(queue_number), 0) + 1 INTO v_queue_number
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id
    AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date;

  -- 9. Determine initial status
  v_is_staff := (auth.uid() IS NOT NULL) AND public.is_active_staff();
  IF v_is_staff THEN
    v_initial_status := 'CONFIRMED';
  ELSE
    v_initial_status := 'PENDING_REVIEW';
  END IF;

  -- 10. Unique reference number
  v_ref := trim(COALESCE(p_reference_number, ''));
  IF v_ref = '' OR v_ref !~ '^BSCH-[0-9]{4,6}$' OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.appointments WHERE reference_number = v_ref) THEN
    LOOP
      v_ref := 'BSCH-' || (floor(random() * 9000) + 1000)::text;
      EXIT WHEN NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.appointments WHERE reference_number = v_ref);
    END LOOP;
  END IF;

  -- 11. Insert record
  INSERT INTO public.appointments (
    reference_number,
    clinic_id,
    clinic_name,
    patient_name,
    phone_number,
    patient_age,
    patient_address,
    appointment_date,
    appointment_time,
    status,
    queue_number,
    notes
  ) VALUES (
    v_ref,
    p_clinic_id,
    v_clinic.name,
    v_clean_name,
    v_clean_phone,
    trim(p_patient_age),
    trim(p_patient_address),
    p_appointment_date,
    COALESCE(trim(p_appointment_time), '09:00'),
    v_initial_status,
    v_queue_number,
    CASE WHEN v_is_staff THEN 'تسجيل حضوري مباشر من مكتب الاستقبال' ELSE NULL END
  ) RETURNING * INTO v_new_appointment;

  -- 12. Insert status history
  INSERT INTO public.appointment_status_history (
    appointment_id,
    status,
    note,
    updated_by
  ) VALUES (
    v_new_appointment.id,
    v_initial_status,
    CASE
      WHEN v_is_staff THEN 'تم تسجيل وتأكيد الحجز فورياً عبر مكتب الاستقبال'
      ELSE 'تم تسجيل طلب الحجز بنجاح عبر البوابة الإلكترونية وبانتظار المراجعة'
    END,
    CASE WHEN v_is_staff THEN 'موظف الاستقبال' ELSE 'بوابة الحجز الإلكترونية' END
  );

  -- 13. DATA MINIMIZATION: Return ONLY minimal fields to the client
  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'appointment', jsonb_build_object(
      'reference_number', v_new_appointment.reference_number,
      'clinic_id', v_new_appointment.clinic_id,
      'clinic_name', v_new_appointment.clinic_name,
      'patient_name', v_new_appointment.patient_name,
      'phone_number', v_new_appointment.phone_number,
      'appointment_date', v_new_appointment.appointment_date,
      'appointment_time', v_new_appointment.appointment_time,
      'status', v_new_appointment.status,
      'queue_number', v_new_appointment.queue_number
    )
  );
END;
$$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 6. Hardened Two-Factor Tracking RPC Function (track_appointment_secure)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Strictly requires BOTH the Reference Number AND the matching Phone Number.
-- Rejects queries with only phone or only reference.
-- Returns ONLY minimal tracking fields (TABLE of 8 fields), preventing any
-- exposure of patient address, age, medical notes, or unmasked database internals.
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT, TEXT);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(
  p_reference_number TEXT,
  p_phone_number TEXT
)
RETURNS TABLE (
  reference_number TEXT,
  clinic_id TEXT,
  clinic_name TEXT,
  patient_name TEXT,
  appointment_date DATE,
  appointment_time TEXT,
  status appointment_status,
  queue_number INTEGER
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_clean_ref TEXT;
  v_clean_phone TEXT;
BEGIN
  -- 1. Validate both inputs exist
  v_clean_ref := trim(upper(COALESCE(p_reference_number, '')));
  v_clean_phone := regexp_replace(trim(COALESCE(p_phone_number, '')), '\D', '', 'g');

  IF v_clean_ref = '' OR v_clean_phone = '' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  -- Prepend BSCH- if 4-6 digits provided
  IF v_clean_ref ~ '^[0-9]{4,6}$' THEN
    v_clean_ref := 'BSCH-' || v_clean_ref;
  END IF;

  -- Validate reference number format
  IF v_clean_ref !~ '^BSCH-[0-9]{4,6}$' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  -- Normalize phone (handle missing 0)
  IF length(v_clean_phone) = 10 AND v_clean_phone ~ '^(10|11|12|15)' THEN
    v_clean_phone := '0' || v_clean_phone;
  END IF;

  -- Validate Egyptian phone format
  IF v_clean_phone !~ '^01[0125][0-9]{8}$' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  -- 2. Query matching active appointment strictly requiring BOTH ref AND phone
  RETURN QUERY
  SELECT
    a.reference_number,
    a.clinic_id,
    a.clinic_name,
    a.patient_name,
    a.appointment_date,
    a.appointment_time,
    a.status,
    a.queue_number
  FROM public.appointments a
  WHERE a.reference_number = v_clean_ref
    AND a.phone_number = v_clean_phone
  ORDER BY a.appointment_date DESC, a.created_at DESC
  LIMIT 1;

  RETURN;
END;
$$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 7. Function Execution Permissions Hardening
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT, TEXT) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT) TO anon, authenticated, service_role;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_doctor_clinic_id() FROM PUBLIC;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_doctor_clinic_id() TO authenticated, service_role;
