===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\migrations\20261031010000_staff_auth_provisioning.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بدمسنا
-- Migration: 20261031010000_staff_auth_provisioning.sql
-- Purpose: Staff Auth Policies & Profile Verification Helper (Security Hardened)
-- ========================================================================
--
-- CRITICAL SECURITY HARDENING APPLIED:
-- 1. `provision_staff_member` is REVOKED from PUBLIC, anon, and authenticated.
--    Granted ONLY to `service_role` (and runs as postgres in SQL Editor).
-- 2. Added internal role verification guard inside the function body as defense in depth.
-- 3. Required dependency definitions included safely (staff_role enum, get_current_staff_role).
-- 4. Reminded owner to disable public sign-ups in Supabase Auth dashboard.
-- ========================================================================

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- CRITICAL SECURITY REMINDER FOR PROJECT OWNER:
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- In Supabase Dashboard -> Authentication -> Providers (Email) / Auth Settings:
-- 1. YOU MUST DISABLE "Enable Email Signup" / "Allow new users to sign up".
--    Public self-registration for staff accounts must remain completely disabled.
-- 2. DISABLE "Confirm email" (email confirmation off) for internal staff accounts.
-- 3. Staff users in auth.users MUST be created with:
--    email = username@STAFF_AUTH_DOMAIN (default: username@hospital.internal).
-- 4. Staff accounts must ONLY be created by an administrator in Supabase Auth Dashboard.
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- 0. Ensure safe dependencies
DO $$ BEGIN
  CREATE TYPE staff_role AS ENUM ('ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST', 'DOCTOR');
EXCEPTION
  WHEN duplicate_object THEN null;
END $$;

-- Helper: Get current staff user role (Security Definer, Stable, safe search_path)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_current_staff_role()
RETURNS staff_role
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
  SELECT role FROM public.staff_profiles
  WHERE id = auth.uid() AND is_active = true
  LIMIT 1;
$$;

-- 1. Enable RLS on staff_profiles
ALTER TABLE public.staff_profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- 2. Revoke any direct public/anon access
REVOKE ALL ON public.staff_profiles FROM anon;
REVOKE ALL ON public.staff_profiles FROM PUBLIC;

-- 3. Staff can read their own profile (uses direct id match, prevents RLS recursion)
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can read own profile" ON public.staff_profiles;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can read own profile"
  ON public.staff_profiles FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (id = auth.uid());

-- 4. Admins can view all staff profiles
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins can view all staff profiles" ON public.staff_profiles;
CREATE POLICY "Admins can view all staff profiles"
  ON public.staff_profiles FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN');

-- 5. Admins can manage staff profiles
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins can manage staff profiles" ON public.staff_profiles;
CREATE POLICY "Admins can manage staff profiles"
  ON public.staff_profiles FOR ALL
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN')
  WITH CHECK (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN');

-- 6. Helper Function: Idempotent staff profile linkage
-- Can be called via Supabase SQL Editor (postgres) or backend service_role only
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.provision_staff_member(
  p_user_id UUID,
  p_username TEXT,
  p_full_name TEXT,
  p_role staff_role,
  p_clinic_id TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS VOID
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
  -- Defense in depth:
  -- Note: Inside a SECURITY DEFINER function, `current_user` is the function owner (postgres).
  -- To inspect the real invoking session/role, check `session_user` (for direct DB connections)
  -- and `auth.role()` (for Supabase PostgREST API connections).
  IF session_user NOT IN ('postgres', 'supabase_admin')
     AND COALESCE(auth.role(), '') != 'service_role'
     AND COALESCE(public.get_current_staff_role(), 'RECEPTIONIST'::staff_role) != 'ADMIN' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Access denied: Only administrators or service_role can provision staff members.';
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.staff_profiles (id, username, full_name, role, clinic_id, is_active)
  VALUES (p_user_id, p_username, p_full_name, p_role, p_clinic_id, true)
  ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
    username = EXCLUDED.username,
    full_name = EXCLUDED.full_name,
    role = EXCLUDED.role,
    clinic_id = EXCLUDED.clinic_id,
    is_active = true,
    updated_at = timezone('utc'::text, now());
END;
$$;

-- Strictly REVOKE execute privileges from anon and authenticated
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.provision_staff_member(UUID, TEXT, TEXT, staff_role, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.provision_staff_member(UUID, TEXT, TEXT, staff_role, TEXT) FROM anon;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.provision_staff_member(UUID, TEXT, TEXT, staff_role, TEXT) FROM authenticated;

-- GRANT execute privilege ONLY to service_role (SQL editor executes as postgres role directly)
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.provision_staff_member(UUID, TEXT, TEXT, staff_role, TEXT) TO service_role;


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\migrations\20261101000000_clinic_services.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بالبحيرة
-- Migration: 20261101000000_clinic_services.sql
-- Purpose: Dynamic Clinic Services, Service Documents & Hardened Appointment Tracking
-- ========================================================================
--
-- Deliverables in this migration:
-- 1. `public.clinic_services`: Granular service definitions per clinic with
--    dedicated required_documents and instructions.
-- 2. `public.appointments` extensions:
--    - service_id (FK to clinic_services)
--    - service_name (text snapshot)
--    - guardian_national_id (14 digits, confidential, never exposed to anon or public APIs)
--    - booking_code_hash (HMAC-SHA256 hash for CSPRNG booking code printed on receipt)
-- 3. Row Level Security (RLS) on clinic_services (public SELECT active, ADMIN manage).
-- 4. Initial seed of standard pediatric clinic services (marked DRAFT for clinical review).
-- ========================================================================

-- Enable core extensions
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Create Clinic Services Table
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.clinic_services (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT ('srv_' || substr(md5(random()::text), 1, 8)),
  clinic_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES public.clinics(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  required_documents TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  instructions TEXT,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  display_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clinic_services_clinic_id ON public.clinic_services(clinic_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clinic_services_active ON public.clinic_services(is_active);

-- Updated at trigger
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_clinic_services_updated_at ON public.clinic_services;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_clinic_services_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.clinic_services
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_updated_at();

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Extend Appointments Table
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE public.appointments
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS service_id TEXT REFERENCES public.clinic_services(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS service_name TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS guardian_national_id VARCHAR(14),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS booking_code_hash TEXT;

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.appointments.guardian_national_id IS 'Optional 14-digit Egyptian national ID of child guardian. Confidential: strictly forbidden from being returned by public tracking APIs or printed on receipts.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.appointments.booking_code_hash IS 'HMAC-SHA256 hash of the CSPRNG booking code printed on the patient receipt ticket.';

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Row Level Security (RLS) Policies on clinic_services
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE public.clinic_services ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Grants
GRANT SELECT ON public.clinic_services TO anon, authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.clinic_services TO authenticated;

-- Public can view active services
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Public can view active clinic services" ON public.clinic_services;
CREATE POLICY "Public can view active clinic services"
  ON public.clinic_services FOR SELECT
  TO anon, authenticated
  USING (is_active = true OR public.get_current_staff_role() IS NOT NULL);

-- Admin can manage clinic services
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admin can manage clinic services" ON public.clinic_services;
CREATE POLICY "Admin can manage clinic services"
  ON public.clinic_services FOR ALL
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN')
  WITH CHECK (public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN');

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. Initial Seed for Clinic Services Notice
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- NOTE: Demo/provisional services have been intentionally moved OUT of this migration
-- to `supabase/seed/clinic_services_DRAFT.sql` with `is_active = false`.
-- This migration creates only the table structure, constraints, indexes, and RLS policies.




===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\migrations\20261101010000_security_privacy_hardening_rpc_and_patient_tables.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بالبحيرة
-- Migration: 20261101010000_security_privacy_hardening_rpc_and_patient_tables.sql
-- Purpose: 
--   1. Revoke public/anon RPC execution on booking and tracking (service_role only).
--   2. Revoke all direct table access on patient tables from anon and authenticated (service_role only).
--   3. Upgrade unique booking constraint to permit siblings with same phone but different names.
--   4. Implement persistent serverless rate limiting table and atomic RPC.
-- ========================================================================

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Revoke Public & Anon RPC Execution on Sensitive Functions
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- The Next.js server handles all booking and tracking requests using createAdminClient()
-- (service_role). No browser/client calls RPC directly with the anon key.
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT) FROM anon;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT) FROM authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT) TO service_role;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT, TEXT) FROM anon;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT, TEXT) FROM authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.track_appointment_secure(TEXT, TEXT) TO service_role;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Revoke Direct Table Access from anon & authenticated on Patient Data Tables
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Note: A column-level REVOKE does not take effect when a table-level GRANT exists.
-- Therefore, all table-level grants on sensitive patient tables are strictly revoked.
-- All staff and patient reads/writes go through Next.js server routes via service_role.
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.appointments FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.appointment_status_history FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.audit_logs FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.otp_verifications FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

-- Ensure service_role has unhindered administrative access
GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.appointments TO service_role;
GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.appointment_status_history TO service_role;
GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.audit_logs TO service_role;
GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.otp_verifications TO service_role;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Upgrade Booking Uniqueness: Allow Siblings While Blocking Duplicates
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- The previous constraint indexed (clinic_id, phone_number, appointment_date),
-- which inadvertently blocked parents booking for two siblings on the same day.
-- The upgraded index incorporates normalized patient_name to allow distinct siblings.
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS public.idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_clinic_day;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_patient_clinic_day
  ON public.appointments (clinic_id, phone_number, lower(trim(patient_name)), appointment_date)
  WHERE (status != 'CANCELLED');

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. Persistent Rate Limiting Table & Atomic RPC for Serverless
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.rate_limit_counters (
  key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  reset_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

ALTER TABLE public.rate_limit_counters ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.rate_limit_counters FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.rate_limit_counters TO service_role;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.check_rate_limit(
  p_key TEXT,
  p_max_requests INTEGER,
  p_window_seconds INTEGER
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_now TIMESTAMPTZ := timezone('utc'::text, now());
  v_record RECORD;
  v_reset_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
  v_retry_after INTEGER;
BEGIN
  -- Cleanup expired rate limit counters
  DELETE FROM public.rate_limit_counters WHERE reset_at < v_now;

  -- Acquire lock on the specific rate-limiting key
  SELECT * INTO v_record FROM public.rate_limit_counters WHERE key = p_key FOR UPDATE;

  IF NOT FOUND OR v_record.reset_at < v_now THEN
    v_reset_at := v_now + (p_window_seconds || ' seconds')::INTERVAL;
    INSERT INTO public.rate_limit_counters (key, count, reset_at)
    VALUES (p_key, 1, v_reset_at)
    ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE
    SET count = 1, reset_at = EXCLUDED.reset_at;

    RETURN jsonb_build_object('allowed', true, 'remaining', p_max_requests - 1);
  END IF;

  IF v_record.count >= p_max_requests THEN
    v_retry_after := CEIL(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (v_record.reset_at - v_now)))::INTEGER;
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
      'allowed', false,
      'retry_after_seconds', GREATEST(v_retry_after, 1)
    );
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.rate_limit_counters
  SET count = count + 1
  WHERE key = p_key;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object('allowed', true, 'remaining', p_max_requests - (v_record.count + 1));
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.check_rate_limit(TEXT, INTEGER, INTEGER) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.check_rate_limit(TEXT, INTEGER, INTEGER) TO service_role;



===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\migrations\20261101020000_clinic_services_capacity_and_schedules.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بالبحيرة
-- Migration: 20261101020000_clinic_services_capacity_and_schedules.sql
-- Purpose:
--   1. Ensure prerequisite tables/columns exist safely (clinic_services, appointments extensions, rate_limit_counters).
--   2. Dedicated sequence for concurrent reference numbers (seq_appointment_reference_number).
--   3. Safely replace old unique active appointment index (idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_clinic_day)
--      with normalized patient-name composite index (idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_patient_clinic_day)
--      to support sibling bookings while blocking true duplicate bookings.
--      (Creates new index first, verifies presence, then drops old index).
--   4. Add per-service daily capacity (`clinic_services.max_daily_capacity`).
--   5. Add clinic-level required documents (`clinics.required_documents`).
--   6. Rate limit table, periodic cleanup function, and atomic upsert with fail-closed semantics.
--   7. Enhanced atomic booking RPC `book_appointment_atomic`:
--      - Fix uninitialized `v_service` RECORD error by using an independently initialized `v_service_name` variable.
--      - Preserve authenticated staff identity through `service_role` via `p_staff_user_id UUID` parameter,
--        verified strictly against `public.staff_profiles` (active, ADMIN or RECEPTIONIST).
--      - Enforces receptionist clinic confinement: receptionists assigned to a clinic cannot book in other clinics.
--      - Correctly writes to `appointment_status_history.updated_by TEXT` matching base schema.
--      - Uses sequence-based reference number with unique constraint violation retry loop.
--      - Enforce strict calendar exception precedence:
--        Exception (is_closed=true -> closed; is_closed=false -> open with capacity) overrides weekly schedule.
--      - Enforces both clinic daily capacity and per-service daily capacity atomically under advisory xact lock.
--      - Unifies booking horizon strictly to 30 days.
--   8. Phone-only tracking secure RPC with data minimization.
-- ========================================================================

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Prerequisite Schema Extensions & Sequence
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Ensure clinic_services table exists
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.clinic_services (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  clinic_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES public.clinics(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  duration_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 15,
  required_documents TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{"أصل شهادة ميلاد الطفل أو بطاقة التأمين الصحي"}',
  instructions TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'الحضور صباحاً والتسجيل بأسبقية الحضور لشباك الاستقبال بالمستشفى.',
  max_daily_capacity INTEGER NULL,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  display_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

-- Add max_daily_capacity to clinic_services if not present
ALTER TABLE public.clinic_services
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS max_daily_capacity INTEGER NULL;

DO $$ BEGIN
  ALTER TABLE public.clinic_services
    ADD CONSTRAINT chk_service_max_daily_capacity
    CHECK (max_daily_capacity IS NULL OR max_daily_capacity >= 0);
EXCEPTION
  WHEN duplicate_object THEN null;
END $$;

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.clinic_services.max_daily_capacity IS
  'Optional daily capacity ceiling for this specific service. NULL means no separate cap beyond clinic capacity.';

-- Add required_documents array to clinics
ALTER TABLE public.clinics
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS required_documents TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}';

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.clinics.required_documents IS
  'List of general required documents and identity papers for attending this clinic.';

-- Ensure appointments has required extension columns
ALTER TABLE public.appointments
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS service_id TEXT REFERENCES public.clinic_services(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS service_name TEXT,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS guardian_national_id VARCHAR(14);

DO $$ BEGIN
  ALTER TABLE public.appointments
    ADD CONSTRAINT chk_guardian_national_id
    CHECK (guardian_national_id IS NULL OR guardian_national_id ~ '^[0-9]{14}$');
EXCEPTION
  WHEN duplicate_object THEN null;
END $$;

-- Audit and update CHECK constraint on appointments.reference_number to support up to 10 digits
DO $$ BEGIN
  ALTER TABLE public.appointments DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS chk_reference_number;
  ALTER TABLE public.appointments
    ADD CONSTRAINT chk_reference_number
    CHECK (reference_number ~ '^BSCH-[0-9]{4,10}$');
EXCEPTION
  WHEN duplicate_object THEN null;
END $$;

-- Sequence for concurrent, collision-free appointment reference numbers
CREATE SEQUENCE IF NOT EXISTS public.seq_appointment_reference_number
  START WITH 1000
  INCREMENT BY 1;

-- Synchronize sequence safely: only advance forward, NEVER rollback or setval backwards
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_max_ref BIGINT;
  v_curr_seq BIGINT;
BEGIN
  -- Calculate max numeric reference number present in appointments
  SELECT COALESCE(MAX(SUBSTRING(reference_number FROM '^BSCH-([0-9]+)$')::BIGINT), 999)
  INTO v_max_ref
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE reference_number ~ '^BSCH-[0-9]+$';

  -- Safely check current sequence last_value
  SELECT last_value INTO v_curr_seq
  FROM pg_sequences
  WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND sequencename = 'seq_appointment_reference_number';

  -- Only advance forward if max existing reference exceeds current sequence position
  IF v_max_ref > COALESCE(v_curr_seq, 999) THEN
    PERFORM setval('public.seq_appointment_reference_number', v_max_ref, true);
  END IF;
END $$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Safe Index Swap: Sibling Bookings vs Duplicate Child Bookings
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Create replacement index incorporating normalized patient_name FIRST
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_patient_clinic_day
  ON public.appointments (clinic_id, phone_number, lower(regexp_replace(trim(patient_name), '\s+', ' ', 'g')), appointment_date)
  WHERE (status != 'CANCELLED');

-- Safely verify new index creation via pg_index before dropping old index
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_is_valid BOOLEAN := false;
  v_indexdef TEXT;
BEGIN
  -- Deep inspection of pg_index and index definition
  SELECT (i.indisvalid AND i.indisready AND i.indisunique),
         pg_get_indexdef(c.oid)
  INTO v_is_valid, v_indexdef
  FROM pg_index i
  JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = i.indexrelid
  JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace
  WHERE n.nspname = 'public'
    AND c.relname = 'idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_patient_clinic_day';

  -- Verify validity, readiness, uniqueness, patient_name expression, and cancellation predicate
  IF v_is_valid = true 
     AND v_indexdef ILIKE '%patient_name%'
     AND v_indexdef ILIKE '%regexp_replace%'
     AND (v_indexdef ILIKE '%status%CANCELLED%' OR v_indexdef ILIKE '%status <> ''CANCELLED''%' OR v_indexdef ILIKE '%status != ''CANCELLED''%')
  THEN
    DROP INDEX IF EXISTS public.idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_clinic_day;
  ELSE
    RAISE WARNING 'Safety check: New index idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_patient_clinic_day is not fully valid or does not match required expressions. Preserving old index.';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Persistent Rate Limiting Table & RPCs
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.rate_limit_counters (
  key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  reset_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

ALTER TABLE public.rate_limit_counters ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
REVOKE ALL ON TABLE public.rate_limit_counters FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.rate_limit_counters TO service_role;

-- Rate limit opportunistic cleanup function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.cleanup_expired_rate_limits()
RETURNS INTEGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_deleted INTEGER;
BEGIN
  DELETE FROM public.rate_limit_counters
  WHERE reset_at < (clock_timestamp() - INTERVAL '1 hour');
  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_deleted = ROW_COUNT;
  RETURN v_deleted;
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.cleanup_expired_rate_limits() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.cleanup_expired_rate_limits() TO service_role;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.check_rate_limit(
  p_key TEXT,
  p_max_requests INTEGER,
  p_window_seconds INTEGER
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_now TIMESTAMPTZ := clock_timestamp();
  v_reset_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
  v_current_count INTEGER;
  v_retry_after INTEGER;
BEGIN
  -- Opportunistic cleanup roughly 1% of calls to prevent table bloat
  IF random() < 0.01 THEN
    PERFORM public.cleanup_expired_rate_limits();
  END IF;

  -- Upsert rate limit counter
  INSERT INTO public.rate_limit_counters (key, count, reset_at)
  VALUES (p_key, 1, v_now + (p_window_seconds || ' seconds')::interval)
  ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE
  SET 
    count = CASE 
      WHEN public.rate_limit_counters.reset_at <= v_now THEN 1 
      ELSE public.rate_limit_counters.count + 1 
    END,
    reset_at = CASE 
      WHEN public.rate_limit_counters.reset_at <= v_now THEN v_now + (p_window_seconds || ' seconds')::interval 
      ELSE public.rate_limit_counters.reset_at 
    END
  RETURNING count, reset_at INTO v_current_count, v_reset_at;

  IF v_current_count > p_max_requests THEN
    v_retry_after := GREATEST(1, CEIL(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (v_reset_at - v_now)))::INTEGER);
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
      'allowed', false,
      'retry_after_seconds', v_retry_after,
      'current_count', v_current_count
    );
  END IF;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'allowed', true,
    'current_count', v_current_count
  );
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.check_rate_limit(TEXT, INTEGER, INTEGER) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.check_rate_limit(TEXT, INTEGER, INTEGER) TO service_role;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. Enhanced Atomic Booking Function: book_appointment_atomic
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Drop all previous function overloads safely
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, UUID);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(
  p_clinic_id TEXT,
  p_patient_name TEXT,
  p_phone_number TEXT,
  p_patient_age TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_patient_address TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_appointment_date DATE DEFAULT NULL,
  p_appointment_time TEXT DEFAULT '09:00',
  p_reference_number TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_service_id TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_guardian_national_id TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_staff_user_id UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_clinic RECORD;
  v_service RECORD;
  v_service_name TEXT := 'كشف واستشارة طبية';
  v_schedule RECORD;
  v_effective_capacity INTEGER;
  v_current_clinic_booked INTEGER;
  v_current_service_booked INTEGER;
  v_queue_number INTEGER;
  v_ref TEXT;
  v_new_appointment RECORD;
  v_is_staff BOOLEAN := FALSE;
  v_staff_record RECORD;
  v_staff_name TEXT := NULL;
  v_initial_status appointment_status;
  v_clean_phone TEXT;
  v_clean_name TEXT;
  v_normalized_name TEXT;
  v_dow INTEGER;
  v_retries INTEGER := 0;
  v_today_cairo DATE := (timezone('Africa/Cairo', now()))::date;
  v_next_val BIGINT;
  v_constraint_name TEXT;
BEGIN
  -- 1. Clean & validate inputs
  v_clean_phone := regexp_replace(trim(COALESCE(p_phone_number, '')), '\s+', '', 'g');
  v_clean_name := trim(COALESCE(p_patient_name, ''));
  v_normalized_name := lower(regexp_replace(v_clean_name, '\s+', ' ', 'g'));

  IF v_clean_phone !~ '^01[0125][0-9]{8}$' THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'رقم الهاتف غير صحيح. يجب أن يتكون من 11 رقماً ويبدأ بـ (010, 011, 012, 015).');
  END IF;

  IF length(v_clean_name) < 5 OR array_length(regexp_split_to_array(v_clean_name, '\s+'), 1) < 2 THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'يرجى إدخال اسم المريض ثنائي على الأقل.');
  END IF;

  IF p_appointment_date IS NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'تاريخ الموعد مطلوب.');
  END IF;

  IF p_appointment_date < v_today_cairo THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'لا يمكن حجز موعد في تاريخ سابق.');
  END IF;

  -- Unified 30-day booking horizon according to Cairo calendar
  IF p_appointment_date > (v_today_cairo + INTERVAL '30 days') THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'الحجز الإلكتروني متاح بحد أقصى 30 يوماً مقدماً.');
  END IF;

  -- 2. Concurrency Safety: Transactional Advisory Lock per (clinic_id, appointment_date)
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('booking_' || p_clinic_id || '_' || p_appointment_date::text));

  -- 3. Validate Clinic
  SELECT * INTO v_clinic FROM public.clinics WHERE id = p_clinic_id;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المختارة غير موجودة.');
  END IF;

  IF NOT v_clinic.is_active THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المختارة غير مفعلة للحجز حالياً.');
  END IF;

  -- 4. Calendar Exceptions & Weekly Schedule Precedence
  -- Precedence:
  -- Tier 1: Check clinic_schedules for (clinic_id, appointment_date)
  --   - If found & is_closed = true: Closed (holiday/closure), overrides weekly schedule.
  --   - If found & is_closed = false: Open (exceptional opening day), overrides weekly schedule.
  -- Tier 2: If no exception record found:
  --   - Check day of week against clinic.days_of_week.
  SELECT * INTO v_schedule FROM public.clinic_schedules
  WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id AND schedule_date = p_appointment_date;

  IF FOUND THEN
    IF v_schedule.is_closed THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object(
        'success', false,
        'error', 'العيادة مغلقة في هذا اليوم' || COALESCE(': ' || v_schedule.closure_reason, ' نظراً لعطلة رسمية أو ظرف طارئ.')
      );
    END IF;
    -- Exception opening day: capacity override if set, else clinic default
    v_effective_capacity := COALESCE(v_schedule.max_capacity, v_clinic.max_daily_slots);
  ELSE
    -- No schedule exception: apply weekly schedule
    v_dow := EXTRACT(DOW FROM p_appointment_date)::INTEGER;
    IF NOT (v_dow = ANY(v_clinic.days_of_week)) THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة لا تعمل في هذا اليوم (عطلة أسبوعية).');
    END IF;
    v_effective_capacity := v_clinic.max_daily_slots;
  END IF;

  -- 5. Duplicate Reservation Check: Permits siblings with same phone but distinct patient names
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.appointments
    WHERE phone_number = v_clean_phone
      AND clinic_id = p_clinic_id
      AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date
      AND lower(regexp_replace(trim(patient_name), '\s+', ' ', 'g')) = v_normalized_name
      AND status != 'CANCELLED'
  ) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'يوجد حجز نشط مسبقاً بنفس رقم الهاتف لنفس المريض في هذه العيادة في هذا اليوم.');
  END IF;

  -- 6. Clinic Daily Capacity Check
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_current_clinic_booked
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id
    AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date
    AND status != 'CANCELLED';

  IF v_current_clinic_booked >= v_effective_capacity THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
      'success', false,
      'error', format('عفواً، اكتملت السعة اليومية المتاحة للعيادة في هذا اليوم (%s مريض). يرجى اختيار موعد آخر.', v_effective_capacity)
    );
  END IF;

  -- 7. Service Validation & Service-Level Capacity Check
  IF p_service_id IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT * INTO v_service FROM public.clinic_services
    WHERE id = p_service_id AND clinic_id = p_clinic_id;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'الخدمة المختارة غير تابعة لهذه العيادة.');
    END IF;

    IF NOT v_service.is_active THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'الخدمة المختارة غير مفعلة حالياً.');
    END IF;

    v_service_name := v_service.name;

    -- Enforce per-service capacity if configured
    IF v_service.max_daily_capacity IS NOT NULL THEN
      SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_current_service_booked
      FROM public.appointments
      WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id
        AND service_id = p_service_id
        AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date
        AND status != 'CANCELLED';

      IF v_current_service_booked >= v_service.max_daily_capacity THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
          'success', false,
          'error', format('عفواً، اكتملت السعة اليومية المتاحة لخدمة "%s" في هذا اليوم (%s مريض). يرجى اختيار تاريخ آخر أو خدمة بديلة.', v_service.name, v_service.max_daily_capacity)
        );
      END IF;
    END IF;
  END IF;

  -- 8. Monotonic queue number
  SELECT COALESCE(MAX(queue_number), 0) + 1 INTO v_queue_number
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE clinic_id = p_clinic_id
    AND appointment_date = p_appointment_date;

  -- 9. Determine initial status & staff identity strictly
  -- Verify server-supplied p_staff_user_id or auth.uid() against active staff profiles
  IF p_staff_user_id IS NOT NULL THEN
    -- If invoked directly through PostgREST (not service_role), require auth.uid() match
    IF current_user != 'service_role' AND COALESCE(current_setting('request.jwt.claim.role', true), '') != 'service_role' THEN
      IF auth.uid() IS NULL OR auth.uid() != p_staff_user_id THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح: لا يمكن تحديد هوية موظف مختلفة عن الجلسة الموثقة.');
      END IF;
    END IF;

    SELECT id, full_name, role, clinic_id INTO v_staff_record
    FROM public.staff_profiles
    WHERE id = p_staff_user_id AND is_active = true;
    
    IF FOUND THEN
      IF v_staff_record.role NOT IN ('ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST') THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'المستخدم غير مصرح له بتسجيل الحجوزات المباشرة.');
      END IF;

      -- Receptionist clinic confinement
      IF v_staff_record.role = 'RECEPTIONIST' AND v_staff_record.clinic_id IS NOT NULL AND v_staff_record.clinic_id != p_clinic_id THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'موظف الاستقبال غير مصرح له بالتسجيل في عيادة أخرى غير العيادة المخصصة له.');
      END IF;

      v_is_staff := true;
      v_staff_name := v_staff_record.full_name;
    ELSE
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'حساب الموظف غير صالح أو غير نشط.');
    END IF;
  ELSIF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT id, full_name, role, clinic_id INTO v_staff_record
    FROM public.staff_profiles
    WHERE id = auth.uid() AND is_active = true;

    IF FOUND THEN
      IF v_staff_record.role IN ('ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST') THEN
        IF v_staff_record.role = 'RECEPTIONIST' AND v_staff_record.clinic_id IS NOT NULL AND v_staff_record.clinic_id != p_clinic_id THEN
          RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'موظف الاستقبال غير مصرح له بالتسجيل في عيادة أخرى غير العيادة المخصصة له.');
        END IF;
        v_is_staff := true;
        v_staff_name := v_staff_record.full_name;
      END IF;
    END IF;
  END IF;

  IF v_is_staff THEN
    v_initial_status := 'CONFIRMED';
  ELSE
    v_initial_status := 'PENDING_REVIEW';
  END IF;

  -- 10. Dedicated sequence for concurrent reference numbers (strictly database-generated)
  v_next_val := nextval('public.seq_appointment_reference_number');
  v_ref := 'BSCH-' || CASE WHEN v_next_val < 10000 THEN lpad(v_next_val::text, 4, '0') ELSE v_next_val::text END;

  -- 11. Insert record with concurrency collision retry loop and constraint discrimination
  LOOP
    BEGIN
      INSERT INTO public.appointments (
        reference_number,
        clinic_id,
        clinic_name,
        service_id,
        service_name,
        patient_name,
        phone_number,
        patient_age,
        patient_address,
        guardian_national_id,
        appointment_date,
        appointment_time,
        status,
        queue_number
      ) VALUES (
        v_ref,
        v_clinic.id,
        v_clinic.name,
        p_service_id,
        v_service_name,
        v_clean_name,
        v_clean_phone,
        p_patient_age,
        p_patient_address,
        p_guardian_national_id,
        p_appointment_date,
        COALESCE(p_appointment_time, '09:00'),
        v_initial_status,
        v_queue_number
      )
      RETURNING * INTO v_new_appointment;

      EXIT; -- Insert succeeded
    EXCEPTION WHEN unique_violation THEN
      GET STACKED DIAGNOSTICS v_constraint_name = CONSTRAINT_NAME;

      -- Discriminate between reference number collision and duplicate booking violation
      IF v_constraint_name = 'appointments_reference_number_key' 
         OR v_constraint_name = 'idx_appointments_ref' 
         OR v_constraint_name LIKE '%reference_number%' 
         OR v_constraint_name LIKE '%ref%' THEN
        v_retries := v_retries + 1;
        IF v_retries > 5 THEN
          RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'تعذر توليد رقم مرجعي فريد في الوقت الحالي. يرجى المحاولة مرة أخرى.');
        END IF;
        v_next_val := nextval('public.seq_appointment_reference_number');
        v_ref := 'BSCH-' || CASE WHEN v_next_val < 10000 THEN lpad(v_next_val::text, 4, '0') ELSE v_next_val::text END;
      ELSIF v_constraint_name = 'idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_patient_clinic_day' 
         OR v_constraint_name = 'idx_unique_active_apt_per_phone_clinic_day' THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'يوجد حجز نشط مسبقاً بنفس رقم الهاتف لنفس المريض في هذه العيادة في هذا اليوم.');
      ELSE
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'يوجد تعارض مع حجز مسجل مسبقاً (' || COALESCE(v_constraint_name, 'unique violation') || ').');
      END IF;
    END;
  END LOOP;

  -- 12. Record initial status history with matching updated_by TEXT
  INSERT INTO public.appointment_status_history (
    appointment_id,
    status,
    updated_by,
    note
  ) VALUES (
    v_new_appointment.id,
    v_initial_status,
    COALESCE(v_staff_name, CASE WHEN v_is_staff THEN 'طاقم المستشفى' ELSE 'البوابة الإلكترونية' END),
    CASE WHEN v_is_staff THEN 'تسجيل حضوري مباشر من مكتب الاستقبال' ELSE 'طلب حجز إلكتروني جديد' END
  );

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'appointment', jsonb_build_object(
      'id', v_new_appointment.id,
      'reference_number', v_new_appointment.reference_number,
      'clinic_id', v_new_appointment.clinic_id,
      'clinic_name', v_new_appointment.clinic_name,
      'service_id', v_new_appointment.service_id,
      'service_name', v_new_appointment.service_name,
      'patient_name', v_new_appointment.patient_name,
      'phone_number', v_new_appointment.phone_number,
      'appointment_date', v_new_appointment.appointment_date,
      'appointment_time', v_new_appointment.appointment_time,
      'status', v_new_appointment.status,
      'queue_number', v_new_appointment.queue_number,
      'created_at', v_new_appointment.created_at
    )
  );
END;
$$;

-- Security Grants: Strictly service_role only (server-mediated)
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, UUID) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.book_appointment_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, DATE, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, UUID) TO service_role;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 5. Phone-Only Tracking Function: track_appointments_by_phone
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.track_appointments_by_phone(
  p_phone_number TEXT
)
RETURNS TABLE (
  reference_number TEXT,
  clinic_id TEXT,
  clinic_name TEXT,
  service_id TEXT,
  service_name TEXT,
  patient_name TEXT,
  appointment_date DATE,
  status appointment_status,
  queue_number INTEGER
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_clean_phone TEXT;
  v_today_cairo DATE := (timezone('Africa/Cairo', now()))::date;
BEGIN
  v_clean_phone := regexp_replace(trim(COALESCE(p_phone_number, '')), '\s+', '', 'g');

  IF v_clean_phone !~ '^01[0125][0-9]{8}$' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  RETURN QUERY
  SELECT
    a.reference_number,
    a.clinic_id,
    a.clinic_name,
    a.service_id,
    COALESCE(a.service_name, 'كشف واستشارة طبية') AS service_name,
    a.patient_name,
    a.appointment_date,
    a.status,
    a.queue_number
  FROM public.appointments a
  WHERE a.phone_number = v_clean_phone
    AND a.appointment_date >= v_today_cairo
    AND a.status != 'CANCELLED'
  ORDER BY a.appointment_date ASC;
END;
$$;

-- Security Grants: Strictly service_role only (server-mediated)
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.track_appointments_by_phone(TEXT) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.track_appointments_by_phone(TEXT) TO service_role;


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\migrations\20261101030000_atomic_status_update_and_privilege_hardening.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بالبحيرة
-- Migration: 20261101030000_atomic_status_update_and_privilege_hardening.sql
-- Purpose:
--   1. Harden Trigger `check_appointment_update_permissions`:
--      - Eliminate unsafe `auth.uid() IS NULL` automatic admin bypass.
--      - Require explicit verified session or authenticated role.
--      - Enforce strict immutability of `reference_number` under all circumstances.
--      - Enforce terminal state protection (`COMPLETED`, `CANCELLED`) for non-admins.
--      - Strictly block Receptionists from modifying core patient data or marking COMPLETED.
--      - Strictly isolate Doctors to their active assigned clinic with zero tampering on patient data.
--   2. Atomic Status Update RPC: `update_appointment_status_atomic`:
--      - Executes status update and status history insertion atomically in one transaction.
--      - Row locking (`FOR UPDATE`) eliminates race conditions on `called_count` and state transitions.
--      - Enforces staff verification directly against `public.staff_profiles` (never trusts client roles).
--      - Supports authorized patient self-cancellation strictly via matching verified phone number.
--   3. Atomic Clinic Transfer RPC: `transfer_appointment_clinic_atomic`:
--      - Transactional advisory locking on target clinic and date.
--      - Atomic validation of schedule, active status, capacity, and monotonic queue numbering.
--   4. Hardened RLS Policies on `public.appointments`:
--      - Strictly deny unassigned doctors (`clinic_id IS NULL`) from seeing any appointments.
--      - Confine doctors exclusively to their assigned clinic.
--      - Protect completed appointments from receptionist modification.
-- ========================================================================

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Hardened Trigger: check_appointment_update_permissions
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.check_appointment_update_permissions()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_role staff_role;
  v_doc_clinic TEXT;
  v_is_service_role BOOLEAN;
BEGIN
  -- Determine whether caller is service_role
  v_is_service_role := (
    current_user = 'service_role' 
    OR COALESCE(current_setting('request.jwt.claim.role', true), '') = 'service_role'
  );

  -- 1. Reference number is ALWAYS strictly immutable under all circumstances
  IF OLD.reference_number != NEW.reference_number THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'الرقم المرجعي للحجز غير قابل للتعديل نهائياً.';
  END IF;

  -- 2. If caller is not service_role, enforce strict session & role verification
  IF NOT v_is_service_role THEN
    IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح: لا يمكن تعديل الحجوزات دون جلسة موثقة.';
    END IF;

    v_role := public.get_current_staff_role();
    IF v_role IS NULL THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح: حساب المستخدم غير نشط أو غير مسجل في طاقم العمل.';
    END IF;

    -- If Admin, allow update (immutability of reference_number was already checked above)
    IF v_role = 'ADMIN' THEN
      RETURN NEW;
    END IF;

    -- Terminal state protection for non-admin callers
    IF OLD.status = 'COMPLETED' AND NEW.status != 'COMPLETED' THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'لا يمكن تعديل أو إعادة فتح حجز مكتمل تم الكشف عليه مسبقاً إلا بواسطة مدير النظام.';
    END IF;

    IF OLD.status = 'CANCELLED' AND NEW.status != 'CANCELLED' THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'لا يمكن تعديل أو إعادة فتح حجز ملغي مسبقاً.';
    END IF;

    -- Receptionist role restrictions
    IF v_role = 'RECEPTIONIST' THEN
      -- Receptionist cannot change core identity or appointment slot directly
      IF OLD.patient_name != NEW.patient_name
         OR OLD.phone_number != NEW.phone_number
         OR OLD.clinic_id != NEW.clinic_id
         OR OLD.appointment_date != NEW.appointment_date THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح لموظف الاستقبال بتعديل بيانات المريض أو العيادة أو تاريخ الموعد مباشرة. يلزم إجراء تحويل رسمي أو مراجعة مدير النظام.';
      END IF;

      -- Receptionist cannot complete appointments (medical examination is doctor only)
      IF NEW.status = 'COMPLETED' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'إتمام الكشف الطبي وصرف التذكرة صلاحية طبية مخصصة للأطباء فقط.';
      END IF;
    END IF;

    -- Doctor role restrictions
    IF v_role = 'DOCTOR' THEN
      v_doc_clinic := public.get_doctor_clinic_id();
      IF v_doc_clinic IS NULL OR OLD.clinic_id != v_doc_clinic THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح للطبيب بتعديل مواعيد خاصة بعيادة أخرى أو بدون تعيين عيادة معتمدة.';
      END IF;

      -- Doctor cannot tamper with patient identity or appointment date
      IF OLD.patient_name != NEW.patient_name
         OR OLD.phone_number != NEW.phone_number
         OR OLD.appointment_date != NEW.appointment_date THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'غير مصرح للطبيب بتعديل البيانات الشخصية أو تاريخ الحجز.';
      END IF;
    END IF;
  END IF;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trigger_check_appointment_update_permissions ON public.appointments;
CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_appointment_update_permissions
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.appointments
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.check_appointment_update_permissions();

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Atomic Status Update RPC: update_appointment_status_atomic
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.update_appointment_status_atomic(TEXT, appointment_status, UUID, TEXT);
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.update_appointment_status_atomic(TEXT, appointment_status, UUID, TEXT, TEXT);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.update_appointment_status_atomic(
  p_appointment_id TEXT,
  p_target_status appointment_status,
  p_staff_user_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
  p_note TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
  p_caller_phone TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_caller_id UUID;
  v_staff_record RECORD;
  v_role staff_role;
  v_staff_clinic_id TEXT;
  v_staff_name TEXT;
  v_apt RECORD;
  v_clean_phone TEXT;
  v_now TIMESTAMPTZ := timezone('utc'::text, now());
  v_is_recall BOOLEAN := false;
  v_new_called_count INTEGER;
  v_last_called TIMESTAMPTZ;
  v_rows_updated INTEGER;
  v_history_note TEXT;
BEGIN
  -- 1. Determine & Authenticate Caller Identity
  IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL THEN
    -- Direct Supabase authenticated JWT session
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF p_staff_user_id IS NOT NULL AND p_staff_user_id != auth.uid() THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح: لا يمكن انتحال هوية موظف مختلفة عن الجلسة الموثقة.');
    END IF;
  ELSIF p_staff_user_id IS NOT NULL THEN
    -- Server-mediated service_role call with explicit staff identity
    v_caller_id := p_staff_user_id;
  ELSIF p_target_status = 'CANCELLED' AND p_caller_phone IS NOT NULL THEN
    -- Patient self-cancellation flow
    v_caller_id := NULL;
  ELSE
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح: يلزم توفير جلسة موظف موثقة أو مطابقة رقم الهاتف للإلغاء.');
  END IF;

  -- 2. Load and verify staff credentials if caller is staff
  IF v_caller_id IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT id, username, full_name, role, clinic_id, is_active
    INTO v_staff_record
    FROM public.staff_profiles
    WHERE id = v_caller_id;

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'حساب الموظف غير مسجل بالنظام.');
    END IF;

    IF v_staff_record.is_active != true THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'حساب الموظف موقوف عن العمل أو غير نشط.');
    END IF;

    v_role := v_staff_record.role;
    v_staff_clinic_id := v_staff_record.clinic_id;
    v_staff_name := v_staff_record.full_name;
  END IF;

  -- 3. Lock appointment row FOR UPDATE to serialize concurrent status modifications
  SELECT * INTO v_apt
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE (id = p_appointment_id OR reference_number = p_appointment_id)
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'الموعد المطلوب غير موجود.');
  END IF;

  -- 4. Authorization & Business Rule Checks
  IF v_caller_id IS NULL THEN
    -- Patient self-cancellation verification
    v_clean_phone := regexp_replace(trim(COALESCE(p_caller_phone, '')), '\s+', '', 'g');
    IF v_clean_phone != v_apt.phone_number THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'رقم الهاتف غير مطابق لبيانات الحجز.');
    END IF;

    IF v_apt.status = 'COMPLETED' THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'لا يمكن إلغاء موعد تم الكشف عليه واكتماله مسبقاً.');
    END IF;

    IF v_apt.status = 'CANCELLED' THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'هذا الحجز ملغي مسبقاً بالفعل.');
    END IF;

    v_staff_name := 'ولي الأمر';
    v_history_note := COALESCE(p_note, 'طلب إلغاء من ولي الأمر عبر بوابة المتابعة');
  ELSE
    -- Staff authorization checks
    -- Terminal state protection
    IF v_apt.status = 'COMPLETED' AND p_target_status != 'COMPLETED' AND v_role != 'ADMIN' THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'لا يمكن تعديل أو إعادة فتح حجز مكتمل تم الكشف عليه مسبقاً إلا بواسطة مدير النظام.');
    END IF;

    IF v_apt.status = 'CANCELLED' AND p_target_status != 'CANCELLED' AND v_role != 'ADMIN' THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'لا يمكن إعادة فتح حجز ملغي مسبقاً.');
    END IF;

    -- Receptionist constraints
    IF v_role = 'RECEPTIONIST' THEN
      IF p_target_status = 'COMPLETED' THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'إتمام الكشف الطبي وصرف التذكرة صلاحية طبية مخصصة للأطباء فقط.');
      END IF;

      IF v_staff_clinic_id IS NOT NULL AND v_apt.clinic_id != v_staff_clinic_id THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح لموظف الاستقبال بتعديل مواعيد خاصة بعيادة أخرى.');
      END IF;
    END IF;

    -- Doctor constraints
    IF v_role = 'DOCTOR' THEN
      IF v_staff_clinic_id IS NULL THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح للطبيب غير المعين على عيادة بتعديل المواعيد.');
      END IF;

      IF v_apt.clinic_id != v_staff_clinic_id THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح للطبيب بتعديل مواعيد خاصة بعيادة أخرى غير العيادة المخصصة له.');
      END IF;
    END IF;

    -- State transition validity
    IF v_apt.status = 'PENDING_REVIEW' AND p_target_status NOT IN ('CONFIRMED', 'CANCELLED') THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', format('لا يمكن تحويل حالة الحجز مباشرة من "%s" إلى "%s".', v_apt.status, p_target_status));
    ELSIF v_apt.status = 'CONFIRMED' AND p_target_status NOT IN ('CHECKED_IN', 'CANCELLED', 'PENDING_REVIEW') THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', format('لا يمكن تحويل حالة الحجز مباشرة من "%s" إلى "%s".', v_apt.status, p_target_status));
    ELSIF v_apt.status = 'CHECKED_IN' AND p_target_status NOT IN ('COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED', 'CONFIRMED', 'CHECKED_IN') THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', format('لا يمكن تحويل حالة الحجز مباشرة من "%s" إلى "%s".', v_apt.status, p_target_status));
    END IF;

    -- Check if this is a patient recall (calling patient again into CHECKED_IN)
    v_is_recall := (v_apt.status = 'CHECKED_IN' AND p_target_status = 'CHECKED_IN');
    v_history_note := p_note;
  END IF;

  -- 5. Calculate atomic metrics
  v_new_called_count := CASE 
    WHEN p_target_status = 'CHECKED_IN' THEN COALESCE(v_apt.called_count, 0) + 1 
    ELSE COALESCE(v_apt.called_count, 0) 
  END;

  v_last_called := CASE 
    WHEN p_target_status = 'CHECKED_IN' THEN v_now 
    ELSE v_apt.last_called_at 
  END;

  IF v_is_recall AND (v_history_note IS NULL OR v_history_note = '') THEN
    v_history_note := format('إعادة نداء المريض للمرة #%s', v_new_called_count);
  ELSIF v_history_note IS NULL OR v_history_note = '' THEN
    v_history_note := CASE 
      WHEN p_target_status = 'CONFIRMED' THEN 'تأكيد الحجز ومطابقة الموعد'
      WHEN p_target_status = 'CHECKED_IN' THEN 'تسجيل حضور المريض بالعيادة والنداء'
      WHEN p_target_status = 'COMPLETED' THEN 'إتمام الكشف الطبي وتوقيع التذكرة'
      WHEN p_target_status = 'CANCELLED' THEN 'إلغاء الحجز'
      ELSE 'تحديث حالة الحجز'
    END;
  END IF;

  -- 6. Atomically update appointments table
  UPDATE public.appointments
  SET status = p_target_status,
      last_called_at = v_last_called,
      called_count = v_new_called_count,
      updated_at = v_now
  WHERE id = v_apt.id;

  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_rows_updated = ROW_COUNT;
  IF v_rows_updated != 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'فشل تحديث الحجز في قاعدة البيانات: لم يتأثر أي سجل.';
  END IF;

  -- 7. Atomically record status history in the same transaction
  INSERT INTO public.appointment_status_history (
    appointment_id,
    status,
    note,
    updated_by
  ) VALUES (
    v_apt.id,
    p_target_status,
    v_history_note,
    v_staff_name
  );

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'appointment', jsonb_build_object(
      'id', v_apt.id,
      'reference_number', v_apt.reference_number,
      'clinic_id', v_apt.clinic_id,
      'clinic_name', v_apt.clinic_name,
      'service_id', v_apt.service_id,
      'service_name', v_apt.service_name,
      'patient_name', v_apt.patient_name,
      'phone_number', v_apt.phone_number,
      'appointment_date', v_apt.appointment_date,
      'appointment_time', v_apt.appointment_time,
      'status', p_target_status,
      'queue_number', v_apt.queue_number,
      'last_called_at', v_last_called,
      'called_count', v_new_called_count,
      'updated_at', v_now
    )
  );
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.update_appointment_status_atomic(TEXT, appointment_status, UUID, TEXT, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.update_appointment_status_atomic(TEXT, appointment_status, UUID, TEXT, TEXT) TO authenticated, service_role;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Atomic Clinic Transfer RPC: transfer_appointment_clinic_atomic
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.transfer_appointment_clinic_atomic(
  p_appointment_id TEXT,
  p_target_clinic_id TEXT,
  p_staff_user_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
  p_reason TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS JSONB
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_caller_id UUID;
  v_staff_record RECORD;
  v_apt RECORD;
  v_target_clinic RECORD;
  v_target_schedule RECORD;
  v_effective_capacity INTEGER;
  v_current_target_booked INTEGER;
  v_new_queue_number INTEGER;
  v_dow INTEGER;
  v_now TIMESTAMPTZ := timezone('utc'::text, now());
  v_transfer_note TEXT;
  v_rows_updated INTEGER;
BEGIN
  -- 1. Determine Caller Identity
  IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL THEN
    v_caller_id := auth.uid();
    IF p_staff_user_id IS NOT NULL AND p_staff_user_id != auth.uid() THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح: لا يمكن انتحال هوية موظف مختلفة.');
    END IF;
  ELSIF p_staff_user_id IS NOT NULL THEN
    v_caller_id := p_staff_user_id;
  ELSE
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح: يلزم توفير جلسة موظف موثقة للتحويل.');
  END IF;

  SELECT id, full_name, role, clinic_id, is_active
  INTO v_staff_record
  FROM public.staff_profiles
  WHERE id = v_caller_id;

  IF NOT FOUND OR v_staff_record.is_active != true THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'حساب الموظف غير صالح أو غير نشط.');
  END IF;

  -- 2. Lock appointment row FOR UPDATE
  SELECT * INTO v_apt
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE (id = p_appointment_id OR reference_number = p_appointment_id)
  FOR UPDATE;

  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'الموعد المطلوب تحويله غير موجود.');
  END IF;

  IF v_apt.status IN ('COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED') THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'لا يمكن تحويل حجز مكتمل أو ملغي مسبقاً.');
  END IF;

  -- Doctor and Receptionist clinic boundary checks
  IF v_staff_record.role = 'DOCTOR' AND v_staff_record.clinic_id IS NOT NULL AND v_staff_record.clinic_id != v_apt.clinic_id THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح للطبيب بتحويل مريض مسجل في عيادة أخرى.');
  END IF;

  IF v_staff_record.role = 'RECEPTIONIST' AND v_staff_record.clinic_id IS NOT NULL AND v_staff_record.clinic_id != v_apt.clinic_id THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'غير مصرح لموظف الاستقبال بتحويل مريض مسجل في عيادة أخرى.');
  END IF;

  IF v_apt.clinic_id = p_target_clinic_id THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'الحجز مسجل بالفعل في نفس العيادة المختارة.');
  END IF;

  -- 3. Lock target clinic and appointment date with transactional advisory lock
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('booking_' || p_target_clinic_id || '_' || v_apt.appointment_date::text));

  -- 4. Validate Target Clinic
  SELECT * INTO v_target_clinic
  FROM public.clinics
  WHERE id = p_target_clinic_id;

  IF NOT FOUND OR NOT v_target_clinic.is_active THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المحول إليها غير موجودة أو غير مفعلة.');
  END IF;

  -- Check Target Clinic Operating Schedule & Precedence
  SELECT * INTO v_target_schedule
  FROM public.clinic_schedules
  WHERE clinic_id = p_target_clinic_id AND schedule_date = v_apt.appointment_date;

  IF FOUND THEN
    IF v_target_schedule.is_closed THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المحول إليها مغلقة في هذا اليوم نظراً لظرف طارئ أو عطلة رسمية.');
    END IF;
    v_effective_capacity := COALESCE(v_target_schedule.max_capacity, v_target_clinic.max_daily_slots);
  ELSE
    v_dow := EXTRACT(DOW FROM v_apt.appointment_date)::INTEGER;
    IF NOT (v_dow = ANY(v_target_clinic.days_of_week)) THEN
      RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', 'العيادة المحول إليها لا تعمل في هذا اليوم (عطلة أسبوعية).');
    END IF;
    v_effective_capacity := v_target_clinic.max_daily_slots;
  END IF;

  -- Check Target Clinic Capacity
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_current_target_booked
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE clinic_id = p_target_clinic_id
    AND appointment_date = v_apt.appointment_date
    AND status != 'CANCELLED';

  IF v_current_target_booked >= v_effective_capacity THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('success', false, 'error', format('سعة العيادة المحول إليها مكتملة في هذا اليوم (%s مريض).', v_effective_capacity));
  END IF;

  -- Compute Monotonic Queue Number for Target Clinic
  SELECT COALESCE(MAX(queue_number), 0) + 1 INTO v_new_queue_number
  FROM public.appointments
  WHERE clinic_id = p_target_clinic_id
    AND appointment_date = v_apt.appointment_date;

  v_transfer_note := format('تم التحويل من عيادة [%s] إلى عيادة [%s] برقم دور جديد (#%s). السبب: %s',
    v_apt.clinic_name, v_target_clinic.name, v_new_queue_number, COALESCE(p_reason, 'تحويل طبي داخلي'));

  -- 5. Atomically update appointments table
  UPDATE public.appointments
  SET clinic_id = v_target_clinic.id,
      clinic_name = v_target_clinic.name,
      service_id = NULL,
      service_name = 'كشف واستشارة طبية (تحويل عيادة)',
      queue_number = v_new_queue_number,
      status = 'CONFIRMED',
      updated_at = v_now
  WHERE id = v_apt.id;

  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_rows_updated = ROW_COUNT;
  IF v_rows_updated != 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'فشل تحويل الحجز في قاعدة البيانات.';
  END IF;

  -- 6. Atomically record status history
  INSERT INTO public.appointment_status_history (
    appointment_id,
    status,
    note,
    updated_by
  ) VALUES (
    v_apt.id,
    'CONFIRMED',
    v_transfer_note,
    v_staff_record.full_name
  );

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'success', true,
    'appointment', jsonb_build_object(
      'id', v_apt.id,
      'reference_number', v_apt.reference_number,
      'clinic_id', v_target_clinic.id,
      'clinic_name', v_target_clinic.name,
      'patient_name', v_apt.patient_name,
      'phone_number', v_apt.phone_number,
      'appointment_date', v_apt.appointment_date,
      'status', 'CONFIRMED',
      'queue_number', v_new_queue_number,
      'updated_at', v_now
    )
  );
END;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.transfer_appointment_clinic_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, UUID, TEXT) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.transfer_appointment_clinic_atomic(TEXT, TEXT, UUID, TEXT) TO authenticated, service_role;

-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. Hardened Row Level Security (RLS) Policies on Appointments
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------

REVOKE ALL ON public.appointments FROM anon, PUBLIC;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON public.appointments TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.appointments TO service_role;

-- Staff SELECT Policy:
-- - Admin & Receptionist can view appointments
-- - Doctor can ONLY view appointments for their active assigned clinic (NEVER when clinic_id is null)
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can view appointments" ON public.appointments;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can view appointments"
  ON public.appointments FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() IN ('ADMIN', 'RECEPTIONIST')
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
      )
    )
  );

-- Staff UPDATE Policy:
-- - Admin can update any appointment
-- - Doctor can ONLY update their assigned clinic's appointments
-- - Receptionist can update appointments except those marked COMPLETED
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Staff can update appointments" ON public.appointments;
CREATE POLICY "Staff can update appointments"
  ON public.appointments FOR UPDATE
  TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN'
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
      )
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'RECEPTIONIST'
        AND status != 'COMPLETED'
        AND (
          SELECT clinic_id FROM public.staff_profiles WHERE id = auth.uid()
        ) IS NULL
        OR clinic_id = (
          SELECT clinic_id FROM public.staff_profiles WHERE id = auth.uid()
        )
      )
    )
  )
  WITH CHECK (
    public.is_active_staff() AND (
      public.get_current_staff_role() = 'ADMIN'
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'DOCTOR'
        AND public.get_doctor_clinic_id() IS NOT NULL
        AND clinic_id = public.get_doctor_clinic_id()
      )
      OR (
        public.get_current_staff_role() = 'RECEPTIONIST'
        AND status != 'COMPLETED'
        AND (
          SELECT clinic_id FROM public.staff_profiles WHERE id = auth.uid()
        ) IS NULL
        OR clinic_id = (
          SELECT clinic_id FROM public.staff_profiles WHERE id = auth.uid()
        )
      )
    )
  );



===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\seed\clinic_instructions_DRAFT.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بدمسنا
-- DRAFT — must be reviewed and approved by the hospital's physicians before publishing.
-- File: supabase/seed/clinic_instructions_DRAFT.sql
-- ========================================================================
--
-- IMPORTANT CLINICAL & SAFETY NOTICE:
-- 1. All medical instructions below are DRAFTS prepared for clinical review.
-- 2. Specific fasting durations and medication/stimulant advice have been strictly removed.
-- 3. Any clinical instructions regarding fasting or medication must be confirmed directly
--    by the attending physician or hospital administration.
-- 4. This script strictly uses:
--      WHERE (instructions IS NULL OR trim(instructions) = '')
--    to ensure it NEVER overwrites instructions edited or customized in the CMS.
-- ========================================================================

-- 1. Pediatric Surgery (جراحة الأطفال)
UPDATE public.clinics
SET instructions = 
'• إحضار أصل شهادة ميلاد الطفل ودفتر التطعيمات والبطاقة الصحية.
• إحضار كافة التقارير الطبية والتحاليل المعملية وفحوصات السونار أو الأشعة السابقة إن وُجدت.
• ارتداء الطفل ملابس قطنية فضفاضة ومريحة لتيسير الفحص السريري الدقيق.
• الحضور إلى مكتب الاستقبال قبل الموعد بـ 15 إلى 30 دقيقة لتأكيد الحضور وإنهاء إجراءات التذكرة.
• التعليمات الخاصة بالصيام أو الأدوية تُحدد من الطبيب المعالج، يرجى الاتصال بالمستشفى للتأكد قبل الحضور.'
WHERE id = 'surgery' AND (instructions IS NULL OR trim(instructions) = '');

-- 2. Pediatric Cardiology (قلب الأطفال)
UPDATE public.clinics
SET instructions = 
'• إحضار أصل شهادة ميلاد الطفل أو بطاقة التأمين الصحي.
• إحضار رسم القلب (ECG)، وتقارير الموجات فوق الصوتية (Echo)، وأي فحوصات سابقة إن وُجدت.
• التوجه لغرفة الفحص الأولي بالدور الأرضي لقياس العلامات الحيوية (النبض وضغط الدم) قبل الدخول.
• الحضور قبل الموعد بـ 15 إلى 30 دقيقة لإنهاء إجراءات التسجيل وتأكيد الدور.
• التعليمات الخاصة بالصيام أو الأدوية تُحدد من الطبيب المعالج، يرجى الاتصال بالمستشفى للتأكد قبل الحضور.'
WHERE id = 'cardio' AND (instructions IS NULL OR trim(instructions) = '');

-- 3. Echocardiography Unit (إيكو الأطفال)
UPDATE public.clinics
SET instructions = 
'• إحضار أصل شهادة ميلاد الطفل وتقرير الطبيب المحول للفحص.
• إحضار كافة التقارير والفحوصات القلبية السابقة لمقارنة قياسات عضلة القلب والصمامات.
• ارتداء ملابس خفيفة يسهل فتحها من منطقة الصدر لتيسير التصوير بالموجات فوق الصوتية.
• الحضور في الموعد المحدد مع الحرص على تهدئة الطفل وتجنب إجهاده قبل بدء الفحص.
• التعليمات الخاصة بالصيام أو الأدوية تُحدد من الطبيب المعالج، يرجى الاتصال بالمستشفى للتأكد قبل الحضور.'
WHERE id = 'echo' AND (instructions IS NULL OR trim(instructions) = '');

-- 4. Pediatric Dentistry (طب وجراحة أسنان الأطفال)
UPDATE public.clinics
SET instructions = 
'• إحضار أصل شهادة ميلاد الطفل أو بطاقة التأمين الصحي إن وجدت.
• تنظيف أسنان وفم الطفل جيداً بالفرشاة والمعجون قبل الحضور للعيادة.
• إحضار أي أشعة بانوراما أو صور أشعة سينية موضعية سابقة للأسنان إن توفرت.
• الحضور قبل موعد الكشف بـ 15 إلى 30 دقيقة لإنهاء تسجيل تذكرة الانتظار.
• التعليمات الخاصة بالصيام أو الأدوية تُحدد من الطبيب المعالج، يرجى الاتصال بالمستشفى للتأكد قبل الحضور.'
WHERE id = 'dentistry' AND (instructions IS NULL OR trim(instructions) = '');

-- 5. Gastroenterology & Hepatology (الجهاز الهضمي والكبد)
UPDATE public.clinics
SET instructions = 
'• إحضار أصل شهادة ميلاد الطفل ودفتر المتابعة الصحية.
• إحضار كافة الفحوصات والتحاليل المعملية السابقة (صورة دم، تحاليل براز، وظائف كبد) وأشعة السونار إن وُجدت.
• الحضور قبل الموعد المحدد بـ 15 إلى 30 دقيقة لتنسيق الدخول مع تمريض العيادة.
• التعليمات الخاصة بالصيام أو الأدوية تُحدد من الطبيب المعالج، يرجى الاتصال بالمستشفى للتأكد قبل الحضور.'
WHERE id = 'gi' AND (instructions IS NULL OR trim(instructions) = '');

-- 6. Pediatric Neurology (المخ والأعصاب)
UPDATE public.clinics
SET instructions = 
'• إحضار أصل شهادة ميلاد الطفل ودفتر التطعيمات لمتابعة منحنى التطور الحركي والعصبي.
• إحضار أصل رسم المخ الكهربائي (EEG)، وتقارير وأفلام الرنين المغناطيسي (MRI)، أو الأشعة المقطعية الحديثة.
• إحضار أي تسجيلات فيديو توثيقية لنوبات الحركة غير المعتادة إن كانت متوفرة على هاتف ولي الأمر.
• الحضور قبل الموعد بـ 15 إلى 30 دقيقة.
• التعليمات الخاصة بالصيام أو الأدوية تُحدد من الطبيب المعالج، يرجى الاتصال بالمستشفى للتأكد قبل الحضور.'
WHERE id = 'neuro' AND (instructions IS NULL OR trim(instructions) = '');

-- 7. Audiology Clinic (السمعيات والتخاطب)
UPDATE public.clinics
SET instructions = 
'• إحضار أصل شهادة ميلاد الطفل ونتيجة فحص المسح السمعي لحديثي الولادة إن وُجدت.
• التأكد من نظافة أذن الطفل وخلوها من الالتهابات الحادة أو الإفرازات الظاهرة قبل الفحص.
• الحضور قبل الموعد المحدد بـ 15 إلى 30 دقيقة لتجهيز الطفل للفحص السمعي.
• التعليمات الخاصة بالصيام أو الأدوية تُحدد من الطبيب المعالج، يرجى الاتصال بالمستشفى للتأكد قبل الحضور.'
WHERE id = 'audiology' AND (instructions IS NULL OR trim(instructions) = '');

-- 8. Clinical Nutrition (التغذية العلاجية)
UPDATE public.clinics
SET instructions = 
'• إحضار شهادة ميلاد الطفل ودفتر التطعيمات الموثق به قياسات الوزن والطول منذ الولادة.
• إحضار أحدث تحاليل معملية متوفرة (صورة دم كاملة، نسبة الحديد، الكالسيوم، وفيتامين د).
• الحضور قبل الموعد بـ 15 إلى 30 دقيقة لإنهاء تسجيل الوزن والقياسات الحيوية.
• التعليمات الخاصة بالصيام أو الأدوية تُحدد من الطبيب المعالج، يرجى الاتصال بالمستشفى للتأكد قبل الحضور.'
WHERE id = 'nutrition' AND (instructions IS NULL OR trim(instructions) = '');

===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\supabase\seed.sql =====
-- ========================================================================
-- BSCH Clinics — مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بالبحيرة
-- Seed Data & Initial Outpatient Clinics Provisioning
-- File: supabase/seed.sql
-- ========================================================================
--
-- [تنبيه إداري وهندسي بالغ الأهمية]:
-- 1. البيانات الواردة في هذا الملف (أسماء العيادات، مواقع الأدوار، المواعيد،
--    السعة اليومية، والتعليمات الطبية) هي بيانات أولية استرشادية وتجريبية (Provisional Data)
--    أُعدت لأغراض التطوير وتجربة واجهة الحجز وتدفق النظام.
-- 2. لم يتم اعتماد هذه البيانات بشكل نهائي من إدارة مستشفى الأطفال التخصصي بالبحيرة حتى الآن.
-- 3. تم ضبط الحقل `is_hospital_confirmed = false` لجميع العيادات المدرجة لتمييزها صراحةً.
-- 4. يمكن لمدير النظام (ADMIN) تعديل كافة تفاصيل العيادات وتأكيد اعتمادها رسمياً
--    مباشرة عبر لوحة تحكم الإدارة (Staff Dashboard -> إدارة العيادات).
-- ========================================================================

-- 1. إدراج وتحديث العيادات الاسترشادية (Upsert Outpatient Clinics)
INSERT INTO public.clinics (
  id,
  name,
  description,
  location,
  working_hours,
  days_of_week,
  max_daily_slots,
  slot_duration_minutes,
  instructions,
  is_active,
  is_hospital_confirmed
)
VALUES
  (
    'dentistry',
    'عيادة الأسنان',
    'فحص وعلاج أسنان الأطفال، حشو وقائي، علاج العصب وتقويم الأسنان المبكر',
    'الدور الأرضي - عيادة 1',
    '08:30-14:00',
    '{0,1,2,3,4,6}', -- الأحد، الإثنين، الثلاثاء، الأربعاء، الخميس، السبت
    20,
    30,
    'يرجى إحضار بطاقة التأمين الصحي إن وجدت، مع الحضور قبل الموعد بنصف ساعة.',
    true,
    false
  ),
  (
    'echo',
    'عيادة الإيكو',
    'فحص الموجات فوق الصوتية على قلب الأطفال والكشف المبكر عن عيوب القلب الخلقية',
    'أمام عيادة القلب بالدور الرابع',
    '08:30-14:00',
    '{0,1,2,3,4,6}',
    15,
    30,
    'يلزم إحضار تقرير الطبيب المعالج وأشعة الصدر السابقة إن توفرت.',
    true,
    false
  ),
  (
    'nutrition',
    'عيادة التغذية',
    'متابعة النمو، حساسية الطعام، سوء التغذية، وسمنة ونحافة الأطفال',

-- 2. إرشادات ربط حساب مدير النظام وموظفي المستشفى (Staff Provisioning Guide)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ملاحظة أمنية: في Supabase، يرتبط جدول `public.staff_profiles` بحسابات جدول المصادقة
-- `auth.users` عبر المفتاح الخارجي (Foreign Key `id`).
--
-- لإنشاء حساب المدير العام الأول بأمان على خادم Supabase:
-- الخيار (أ): عبر لوحة تحكم Supabase Dashboard:
-- 1. توجه إلى Authentication -> Users -> Add user (Create user).
-- 2. أدخل بريد المدير (مثلاً: admin@bsch.gov.eg) وكلمة مرور قوية.
-- 3. انسخ الـ User UID الخاص به ثم نفذ الاستعلام التالي في SQL Editor:
--
--    INSERT INTO public.staff_profiles (id, username, full_name, role, is_active)
--    VALUES ('<USER_UID_HERE>', 'admin', 'إدارة نظم المعلومات والعيادات', 'ADMIN', true)
--    ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET role = 'ADMIN', is_active = true;
--
-- الخيار (ب): لربط موظف استقبال أو طبيب:
--    INSERT INTO public.staff_profiles (id, username, full_name, role, clinic_id, is_active)
--    VALUES ('<DOCTOR_UID_HERE>', 'dr.sharif', 'د/ شريف عبد الغني', 'DOCTOR', 'echo', true)
--    ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET role = 'DOCTOR', clinic_id = 'echo';
-- ========================================================================


===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\tests\integration\supabase-rls.test.ts =====
/**
 * BSCH Clinics — Live Supabase RLS Integration Test Suite (Opt-In)
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * 1. This suite is OPT-IN and will ONLY run if TEST_SUPABASE_URL and TEST_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY
 *    are explicitly provided in the environment.
 * 2. It must ONLY be run against a SEPARATE, DEDICATED test Supabase project.
 * 3. It will NEVER run against production credentials.
 *
 * Invariants Verified Against Live Database:
 * - Anonymous client (`anon`) is strictly blocked from reading `appointments`, `audit_logs`, `staff_profiles`, and `otp_verifications`.
 * - Anonymous client (`anon`) can read active `clinics`, `website_settings`, and `homepage_sections`.
 * - Non-admin user cannot execute `provision_staff_member` or insert into `staff_profiles`.
 * - Non-admin user cannot modify CMS tables or upload files to `public-cms-assets` bucket.
 */

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

const testUrl = process.env.TEST_SUPABASE_URL;
const testAnonKey = process.env.TEST_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;
const testServiceKey = process.env.TEST_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;

const isConfigured = Boolean(testUrl && testAnonKey);

test('LIVE SUPABASE RLS INTEGRATION SUITE (Opt-In Check)', { skip: !isConfigured }, async (t) => {
  if (!testUrl || !testAnonKey) {
    return;
  }

  // Safety Guard: Refuse to run against production
  if (
    process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ||
    (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL && testUrl === process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL)
  ) {
    throw new Error('SAFETY ERROR: Integration tests must NOT run against production Supabase instance!');
  }

  const anonClient = createClient(testUrl, testAnonKey, {
    auth: { persistSession: false },
  });

  await t.test('RLS Invariant 1: Anon client is forbidden from SELECT on appointments table', async () => {
    const { data, error } = await anonClient.from('appointments').select('*').limit(5);
    // In Supabase, RLS violations on SELECT return an empty list or a permission denied error
    assert.ok(error !== null || (data && data.length === 0), 'Anon must not access appointments');
  });

  await t.test('RLS Invariant 2: Anon client is forbidden from SELECT on audit_logs table', async () => {
    const { data, error } = await anonClient.from('audit_logs').select('*').limit(5);
    assert.ok(error !== null || (data && data.length === 0), 'Anon must not access audit_logs');
  });

  await t.test('RLS Invariant 3: Anon client is forbidden from SELECT on staff_profiles table', async () => {
    const { data, error } = await anonClient.from('staff_profiles').select('*').limit(5);
    assert.ok(error !== null || (data && data.length === 0), 'Anon must not access staff_profiles');
  });

  await t.test('RLS Invariant 4: Anon client is forbidden from SELECT on otp_verifications table', async () => {
    const { data, error } = await anonClient.from('otp_verifications').select('*').limit(5);
    assert.ok(error !== null || (data && data.length === 0), 'Anon must not access otp_verifications');
  });

  await t.test('RLS Invariant 5: Anon client CAN read active clinics and website settings', async () => {
    const { data: clinics, error: clinicsErr } = await anonClient
      .from('clinics')
      .select('id, name')
      .eq('is_active', true)
      .limit(5);
    assert.equal(clinicsErr, null, 'Active clinics should be readable by anon');

    const { data: settings, error: settingsErr } = await anonClient
      .from('website_settings')
      .select('site_name')
      .limit(1);
    assert.equal(settingsErr, null, 'Website settings should be readable by anon');
  });

  await t.test('RLS Invariant 6: Anon client CANNOT modify website_settings', async () => {
    const { error } = await anonClient
      .from('website_settings')
      .update({ site_name: 'Hacked Site' })
      .eq('id', 'default');
    assert.ok(error !== null, 'Anon must not be allowed to update website_settings');
  });

  await t.test('RLS Invariant 7: Anon client CANNOT upload to public-cms-assets storage bucket', async () => {
    const dummyBlob = new Blob(['test content'], { type: 'text/plain' });
    const { error } = await anonClient.storage
      .from('public-cms-assets')
      .upload('unauthorized_test.txt', dummyBlob);
    assert.ok(error !== null, 'Anon must not be allowed to upload to public-cms-assets bucket');
  });

  await t.test('RLS Invariant 8: Anon client CANNOT execute provision_staff_member RPC', async () => {
    const { error } = await anonClient.rpc('provision_staff_member', {
      p_user_id: '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000',
      p_username: 'attacker',
      p_full_name: 'Attacker User',
      p_role: 'ADMIN',
    });
    assert.ok(error !== null, 'Anon must be forbidden from calling provision_staff_member');
  });
});

  | number
  | boolean
  | null
  | { [key: string]: Json | undefined }
  | Json[];

===== FILE: E:\خاص روبو\bsch-clinics\types\index.ts =====
export type AppointmentStatus =
  | 'PENDING_REVIEW'   // قيد المراجعة
  | 'CONFIRMED'        // مؤكد
  | 'CHECKED_IN'       // حضر المريض (في قائمة النداء)
  | 'COMPLETED'        // مكتمل (تم الكشف)
  | 'CANCELLED';       // ملغي

export interface StatusHistoryItem {
  status: AppointmentStatus;
  timestamp: string;
  note?: string;
  updatedBy?: string;
}

export interface ClinicService {
  id: string;
  clinicId: string;
  name: string;
  requiredDocuments: string[];
  instructions: string;
  maxDailyCapacity?: number;
  isActive: boolean;
  displayOrder: number;
  createdAt?: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

export interface ClinicScheduleException {
  id: string;
  clinicId: string;
  scheduleDate: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  maxCapacity?: number;
  isClosed: boolean;
  closureReason?: string;
  createdAt?: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

export interface Clinic {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  location: string;
  workingHours: string;
  daysOfWeek: number[]; // 0 = Sunday, 1 = Monday, ..., 6 = Saturday (Egypt outpatient usually Sun-Thu or Sat-Thu)
  maxDailySlots: number;
  slotDurationMinutes: number;
  instructions?: string;
  requiredDocuments?: string[];
  services?: ClinicService[];
  isActive: boolean;
  isHospitalConfirmed?: boolean;
}

export interface AppointmentSlot {
  id: string;
  clinicId: string;
  date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  time: string; // HH:mm
  capacity: number;
  bookedCount: number;
  isAvailable: boolean;
}

export interface Appointment {
  id: string;
  referenceNumber: string; // e.g. BSCH-7492
  clinicId: string;
  clinicName: string;
  serviceId?: string;
  serviceName?: string;
  patientName: string;
  phoneNumber: string;
  patientAge?: string;
  patientAddress?: string;
  guardianNationalId?: string;
  bookingCode?: string; // One-time unhashed CSPRNG booking code returned only on creation
  bookingCodeHash?: string;
  requiredDocuments?: string[];
  serviceInstructions?: string;
  appointmentDate: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  appointmentTime: string; // HH:mm
  status: AppointmentStatus;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  notes?: string;
  queueNumber?: number;
  lastCalledAt?: string;
  calledCount?: number;
  isVerified?: boolean;
  statusHistory: StatusHistoryItem[];
}

export type StaffRole = 'ADMIN' | 'RECEPTIONIST' | 'DOCTOR';

export interface StaffUser {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  name: string;
  role: StaffRole;
  clinicId?: string;
  active: boolean;
}

export interface CreateAppointmentInput {
  clinicId: string;
  serviceId?: string;
  serviceName?: string;
  patientName: string;
  phoneNumber: string;
  patientAge?: string;
  patientAddress?: string;
  guardianNationalId?: string;
  notes?: string;
  appointmentDate: string;
  appointmentTime?: string;
  isWalkIn?: boolean;
  staffUserId?: string;
}

export interface AuditLogItem {
  id: string;
  timestamp: string;
  staffName: string;
  staffRole: string;
  action: string;
  details: string;
  referenceNumber?: string;
}

import type {NextConfig} from 'next';

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  reactStrictMode: true,
  eslint: {
    ignoreDuringBuilds: true,
  },
  typescript: {
    ignoreBuildErrors: false,
  },
  // Allow access to remote image placeholder.
  images: {
    remotePatterns: [
      {
        protocol: 'https',
        hostname: 'picsum.photos',
        port: '',
        pathname: '/**', // This allows any path under the hostname
      },
    ],
  },
  output: 'standalone',
  transpilePackages: ['motion'],
  webpack: (config, {dev}) => {
    // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
    // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
    if (dev && process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true') {
      config.watchOptions = {
        ignored: /.*/,
      };
    }
    return config;
  },
};

export default nextConfig;
